Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

</details>


### [69] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COBRA-Skills 将 LLM 智能体的技能优化建模为动态候选空间上的预算化序贯优化，结合上下文老虎机优先级调度与证据驱动技能演化，在六个基准、三种模型上取得最优平均性能，同时将优化成本较 SkillOpt 降低 55–58%，每基准仅用 50 个优化样例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可从历史任务经验蒸馏出的可复用技能中获益，但现有技能优化方法依赖昂贵的基于执行的评估，且需要大量任务数据，导致优化成本高、数据效率低。

Method: 提出 COBRA-Skills 高效框架，将技能优化形式化为动态演化候选空间上的预算化序贯优化问题；将上下文老虎机引导的候选优先级排序与基于证据的技能演化相耦合，选择性地将评估预算分配给有潜力或信息量大的候选，并利用执行反馈持续精炼技能种群。

Result: 在六个异构智能体基准和三种目标模型上，COBRA-Skills 在对比方法中取得最强平均性能；相对 SkillOpt 降低优化成本 55–58%，每个基准仅使用 50 个唯一优化样例。进一步分析表明，该方法对智能体 harness 变化保持鲁棒，且当目标模型自身用于技能生成与精炼时仍有效。

Conclusion: COBRA-Skills 通过预算化序贯决策与证据驱动演化，在显著降低评估成本与数据需求的同时持续提升技能质量，具备跨基准、跨模型、跨 harness 的通用性与自生成适用性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [70] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对多LLM智能体答案冲突问题，提出基于贝叶斯反向推理构造反向后验，通过Jensen-Shannon散度度量跨路径一致性，进而设计硬选择(MinJS)、软重加权(FwdJS)、对数线性融合(LogLin)三种聚合策略，在DDXPlus数据集上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体集体决策方法（投票、选举规则、LLM评判）均依赖前向推理，将证据单向映射为标签，其聚合的估计共享同一“证据到标签”的因子分解，因此前向池内易继承相关误差，难以真正发挥智能体多样性优势。

Method: 对每个实例，借助显式似然通过贝叶斯反向推理构造反向后验；前向与反向后验提供不同因子分解的后验近似。利用Jensen-Shannon散度对智能体进行跨路径一致性排序，并据此设计三种策略：硬选择(MinJS)、软重加权(FwdJS)和对数线性融合(LogLin)；在有标注数据时用轻量两阶段校准进一步优化反向锚点。

Result: 在DDXPlus数据集、五种LLM骨干上评估：MinJS在所有骨干上均优于随机选择；FwdJS总体优于最强基线；LogLin在所有评估方法中表现最佳，且在智能体存在分歧的子集上增益最大。

Conclusion: 尽管反向后验单独准确率较弱，但作为锚点比纯前向方案更有用，为集体决策提供互补信息；跨路径一致性是有效的聚合信号，配合两阶段校准可进一步提升聚合性能。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [71] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SIRF风险基础模型，将平台复杂风控策略通过CPT内化到权重中，在仅判定输出、超高精度与秒级时延约束下实现高自动处置率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业内容风控的真实部署约束不是平均准确率，而是在高精度、秒级时延下能自动处理多少风险，同时避免额外人工标注和长链路推理带来的延迟。

Method: 用EntiGraph与MAGA改写、账号级链式思考（CoT）合成策略数据，经继续预训练（CPT）将策略内化进模型权重；采用同源对照实验（Qwen3-8B-SFT vs SIRF-8B-SFT），策略注入与仅判定输出形式一致，仅差策略驱动的CPT；并以树模型仲裁层形式部署。

Result: SIRF-8B-SFT达到71.3% Black Recall@P95，较基线提升15.1pp，仅用约70M CPT tokens且不损害通用能力；在该接口下与包含的、可提供logprob的更大规模模型相比持平或更优；部署为树模型仲裁层后多召回20%被误罚样本；迁移至冻结场景实现约70%的相对误罚下降。

Conclusion: 策略内化式CPT是低标注成本、低时延、高精度自动风控的有效路径，收益可归因于内化，且具备跨场景迁移与工程部署价值。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [72] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将EOPD与ToDi两种逐token门控信号统一为四系数参数化形式，在TweetEval任务上以Qwen3-32B教师蒸馏Qwen3-4B学生进行探索性比较，发现动态门控在多数格点优于静态基线，但效应量在三seed复现中缩小且不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐token门控前向/反向KL损失已成为在线策略知识蒸馏（OPD）的标准技术，但EOPD与ToDi各自固定单一门控信号与门控方向，二者从未被直接比较；缺乏统一坐标系统来对比不同门控设计。

Method: 提出四系数参数化 lambda_t = sigma(a*h_t + b*u(x) + c + d*gap_t)，使EOPD与ToDi的方向对齐代理成为一维限制条件，并引入多通道组合与显式偏置作为额外自由度。在TweetEval情绪与仇恨任务上，用Qwen3-32B教师蒸馏Qwen3-4B学生，进行36格点全家族与单通道（仅熵/仅gap）1D限制的对比，以及26格点均值匹配隔离实验；并对9个头条比较做三seed配对复现（含offensive任务）。

Result: 全家族配置在36个可比格点中的33个取得高于匹配幅度单通道1D限制的准确率；26格点均值匹配隔离实验中，动态门控在19/26格点优于有效KL匹配的静态基线。三seed配对复现方向一致，但效应量小于单seed估计，n=3下不显著。

Conclusion: 由于格点共享训练数据、模型与参数子结构，上述计数仅作为探索性聚合方向性证据而非独立假设检验。作者主要将该参数化定位为短输出分类OPD中比较逐token门控设计的共享坐标系统。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [73] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对隐藏状态进行逐层干预，论文刻画了语言模型回答问题时对"查询路由信息"与"目标知识"依赖的动态交接过程，并区分了不同路由方向与内容依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在答题过程中，其依赖如何从查询路由信息转向目标知识，这一动态变化此前缺乏清晰刻画；作者希望把"可读性、自然强度、因果操控、后期内容依赖"这几个常被混同的测量维度区分开来。

Method: 在问题末尾的隐藏状态上做逐层干预，覆盖 Qwen、Llama、Gemma 三个模型，比较国家-大洲问题（名词、形容词、代码三类答案）。使用三类区分性测量：配对条件化的请求方向（单国问题中查询哪个国家）、全局请求方向（配对问题中第一国 vs 第二国）、以及针对隐藏状态中已有内容的独立选择候选；并对冻结的 Qwen 自然问题状态进行诊断性再分析。

Result: Qwen 中，配对条件化方向在针对它的干预开始改变后期拟合知识之前逐渐增强，该因果窗口在答案支撑内容仍处于形成期时开启。三模型配对轨迹并不一致：Gemma 呈现部分重叠的中层路由-内容轮廓，Llama 在相同门槛下没有持续的路由效应窗口。配对协议中，对全局请求方向的依赖从固定较早层到较晚层集递减，而对拟合内容的依赖持续存在。匹配的 Qwen 对照显示配对条件化方向仍有后期效应，因此这一操作性交接针对的是全局拟合方向而非全部请求信息。

Conclusion: 模型对查询路由信息与目标知识的依赖存在层间交接，但该交接具有方向特异性和模型特异性；研究由此将早期可读性、自然强度、因果操控与后期内容依赖区分开来。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [74] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Explainability Assistant，一个开源对话式 XAI 系统，利用现代 LLM 的函数调用能力，替代传统 XAI 仪表盘和基于刚性语法的对话系统，在意图解析准确率上从 76.8% 提升至 94%，并支持跨 ML 问题类型的自然语言交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能耗预测依赖日益复杂的机器学习模型（如基于遗传规划的符号回归器），其预测难以被设施管理者和楼宇运营人员解读。传统 XAI 仪表盘需较高技术门槛且灵活性不足，而既有对话式 XAI（如 TalkToModel）受限于定制语法，意图解析准确率仅 76.8%。

Method: 提出 Explainability Assistant：开源对话式 XAI 系统，借助现代 LLM 的函数调用（function-calling）能力实现自然语言交互；无需任务特定微调即可适配不同 ML 问题类型；并开展与能源领域专家的对比评估，将系统与传统 XAI 仪表盘进行对照。

Result: 意图解析准确率达到 94%；支持灵活的自然语言交互；可跨 ML 问题类型自适应。专家评估表明可用性提升、任务准确率稳定，且所有专家一致偏好对话式界面用于实际场景。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 函数调用的对话式 XAI 能有效克服传统仪表盘的可用性瓶颈与定制语法的准确率限制，是面向能源等领域非技术用户的可行解释性交互范式。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [75] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文指出主流线性深度推荐模型本质上只加了核范数或Frobenius范数正则项，并据此提出两种兼具低秩与闭式解优势的新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度推荐算法在标准基准上性能相近、代价函数相似，作者希望探究这种性能可比性是巧合还是可被统一到单一框架下。

Method: 分析多类线性性能领先模型的等价形式，发现其等价于核范数正则或Frobenius范数正则；前者结构僵硬、预测能力受限但低秩且有闭式解，后者表达力与效率更强但解为满秩或需ADMM等难调数值过程；随后通过推广Frobenius范数正则提出两种低秩闭式解。

Result: 验证了性能领先者可分为核范数正则和Frobenius范数正则两类，并给出融合两者优点的低秩闭式解。

Conclusion: 深度推荐模型的可比性能并非偶然，可统一在正则化框架下；新提出的低秩闭式解在表达力与求解效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [76] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MindTopo 基准，基于认知科学与形式拓扑的五大拓扑性质（连续性、分离性、序、包围、纽结），从推理与规划两个认知层次评估多模态大模型的拓扑直觉，覆盖 13 类程序化生成任务、11,030 个实例，并评测 14 个 MLLM，发现模型普遍"推理强于规划"，且最佳模型仍远低于人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖距离、角度、形状等度量属性，还依赖在连续变形下保持不变的拓扑关系。认知科学视拓扑关系为空间理解的基础，但现有基础模型评测大多聚焦于度量或视角依赖的关系，忽视了拓扑直觉这一更根本的能力维度，因此缺乏系统性评测手段。

Method: 构建 MindTopo 基准：涵盖连续性、分离性、序、包围、纽结五种拓扑性质，每种性质在"推理"与"规划"两个认知层次上评估。推理任务要求模型识别拓扑关系或推断其变化；规划任务将基础模型实例化为闭环智能体，由其策略选择环境动作。基准包含 13 类程序化生成任务、难度可控，共 11,030 个实例。评测 14 个 MLLM，并研究引入图像与视频生成增强的智能体配置（含 3 个视频生成模型用于规划场景）。

Result: 所有 MLLM 在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务；最佳模型仍远低于人类观测表现。在 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于对规划的提升。生成的观测能保留局部线索并到达看似合理的终点，但经审计的 rollout 无法可靠遵循环境动力学，也无法在状态转移中保持拓扑性质。

Conclusion: MindTopo 揭示了当前多模态基础模型在拓扑直觉上的显著缺陷，尤其在闭环规划与状态转移中的拓扑保持方面；该基准为评估和推动模型的拓扑空间理解提供了系统化工具与诊断依据。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [77] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"人工本我"（artificial id）——一种决定行为应继续、停止或改变的自适应内部驱动力；在微型虚拟培养皿实验中，一个无法进行通用推理、且没有任务行为目标的控制器通过"差异持续性"（differential persistence）自发形成了有用的控制能力，但也因此可能让错位、状态损坏与意外行为跨任务边界持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正从受限任务执行转向跨任务边界持续运行并保留关键状态。这一转变带来控制难题：当前框架多以手工方式从外部规定目标、重试、验证、停止规则等行为转换。作者希望探究行为方向性是否可以在不被显式指定为行为目标的情况下自适应地涌现，并关注这种持续性带来的对齐风险。

Method: 设计了一个最小化的虚拟培养皿实验。控制器规模极小，不具备通用推理能力，也不接收任何任务特定的行为目标。核心机制是"差异持续性"：依据哪些行为更能持续存在来筛选控制策略。

Result: 同一机制在某种物理策略持续更优时，会选中一个非预期的物理策略；当环境的语义发生变化时，它又能替换已学到的传感器映射。说明自适应方向性无需被显式定义为行为目标即可涌现。

Conclusion: 令自适应能动性有用的同一种持续性，也会让错位、被污染的状态和意外行为跨任务边界延续。可扩展的人工本我将在跨边界时携带关键状态与自适应驱动力，从而使对齐成为持续存在的智能体系统的属性，而非单次模型响应或单条轨迹的属性。此类系统需要在可信观测、后果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、来源与硬约束之上建立持久对齐边界。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [78] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文评估2–8B可本地部署的视觉语言模型（VLM）在物种识别中的分类学知识，发现所有模型在相机陷阱图像上均严重退化，且远不及300M的专用模型BioCLIP；退化源于图像可读性而非细粒度判别失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常部署于算力受限、连接性差的边缘设备，因此实际可用的模型是小型本地可部署VLM而非前沿大模型。作者旨在检验这一2–8B部署相关区间的模型是否具备真正的分类学知识，并厘清性能差异究竟来自模型规模还是专用训练数据。

Method: 在96物种任务上评估四个2–8B VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与300M专用模型BioCLIP；对比干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集的相机陷阱图像；在两个独立采样的评估集上测试，并在扩展的200图像样本上比较，同时进行开放集提示以考察虚构物种名现象。

Result: 所有模型均显著高于随机水平，但在野外图像上急剧退化（域差距9.6–26.6个百分点，跨分类层级与两个评估集一致）。BioCLIP显著优于所有VLM（33.2–59.2个百分点，扩展200图像样本），尽管参数量小得多，说明差距源于专用训练数据而非规模。BioCLIP自身域差距（18.0点）与最佳VLM（22.3点）统计上无差异。开放集提示下5.9–9.6%的回复是语法合法但分类学上不存在的物种名，且虚构率的相对排序在两个评估集上完全复现。

Conclusion: 小规模VLM具备真实但有限的分类学知识；干净到野外的性能退化是图像质量变化的固有属性，而非通用模型的弱点；专用训练数据带来的收益大于模型规模；模型间虚构率的相对排序比单点估计更稳健。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [Your Model Already Knows Don't Teach It, Learn to Ask It: Soft Prompting for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11310)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Siyi Li,Hewei Wang,Cesar Daniel Hernandez,Wei Zhao,Wolfgang M. Pauli,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在跨域少样本目标检测中，仅优化少量连续软提示词元（约7,168参数）即可匹配LoRA微调精度，且不导致预训练能力遗忘，并可迁移、可文本化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs跨域少样本适配主要依赖离散提示搜索与LoRA微调；前者搜索空间离散、后者参数量大且易造成预训练能力遗忘，作者希望重新审视被忽视的软提示方案。

Method: 冻结预训练骨干，仅优化少量连续提示词元，并系统研究两个设计选择：提示词元的插入位置（跨模态边界，即视觉与文本词元之间）与初始化方式（空空间词元初始化，优于语义与随机初始化）。

Result: 跨模态边界放置优于其他位置（10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP）；空空间词元初始化最优。1–3个提示词元（平均7,168参数）在Roboflow20-VL（14.2 mAP，10-shot）上匹配最佳LoRA配置，训练参数少20,000倍以上。但优化难度更高、跨随机种子方差更大。不造成遗忘：同精度LoRA使NaturalBench VQA相对下降35%，最大秩达56%，而软提示保持预训练性能不变。词元可迁移到Qwen3.5-9B（+0.8 mAP，无需重训），并可文本化为可读提示，与DetPO持平、优于GEPA。在RoboCasa操作任务上，冻结的π_0.5 VLA策略在梯度瓶颈处放置词元时，三个任务中有两个匹配LoRA基线。

Conclusion: 现代VLMs已编码专业领域所需的大部分知识，适配的关键在于"如何提问"而非重新学习权重；软提示是参数高效、无遗忘、可迁移的替代方案。

Abstract: We address few-shot object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) in out-of-domain settings such as aerial, industrial, and medical imagery, using only ten annotated images for supervision. Existing adaptation methods are discrete prompt optimization and LoRA fine-tuning. We revisit a third option: soft prompting, where a small number of continuous prompt tokens are optimized while the pretrained backbone remains frozen.
  We identify two key design choices. First, placing prompt tokens at the cross-modal boundary between visual and text tokens outperforms other placements (10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP). Second, initializing prompts from the empty space token outperforms semantic and random initialization.
  With these choices, one to three learned tokens (7,168 parameters on average) match the best LoRA configuration on Roboflow20-VL (14.2 mAP, 10-shot) while training over 20,000x fewer parameters. Soft prompting remains harder to optimize, exhibiting higher variance across random seeds. Unlike LoRA, however, it causes no forgetting: the LoRA rank matching our accuracy reduces NaturalBench VQA accuracy by 35% relative, rising to 56% at the largest rank, whereas soft prompting leaves pretrained performance unchanged.
  The learned tokens behave like prompts rather than weights. They transfer to a newer model without retraining (+0.8 mAP on Qwen3.5-9B) and can be verbalized into readable prompts competitive with prompt-search methods (matching DetPO and outperforming GEPA).
  The approach also extends beyond detection. On RoboCasa manipulation tasks, the frozen $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy benefits from soft prompting, matching the LoRA baseline on two of three tasks when tokens are placed at the gradient bottleneck. These results suggest modern VLMs already encode much of what is needed for specialized domains; the challenge is learning how to ask.

</details>


### [80] [Rethinking Handwritten Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10572)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Ashim Shrestha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphemeNet，一个面向多书写系统手写字符识别的统一架构，以两条正交设计轴显式编码笔画几何先验，在14个基准、8种书写系统上以更少参数超越现有基线，确立了"结构先验效率"原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非拉丁手写字符识别（HCR）研究不足，主流方法将其视为通用图像分类，依赖模型规模隐式学习笔画结构，效率低下。作者主张显式地将书写系统的几何规律编码为架构归纳偏置，可同时提升精度并减少参数量。

Method: GraphemeNet由两条正交二元轴控制：轴1通过持久脚手架注入（PSI）实现笔画级几何规律，即在每个编码器阶段用脚本特定的非对称卷积将笔画脚手架以加权残差形式注入，持续将学习特征锚定于字形几何，区别于跳连、辅助损失或注意力重加权；轴2根据字形判别是否需要空间关系推理，在带门控融合的全局平均池化与跨尺度注意力+笔画拓扑模块（STM）之间选择。此外，通用共享$O(n)$复杂度的线性胶囊路由（LCR）。

Result: 在8种书写系统、14个基准上，仅脚手架与解码器拓扑随书写系统变化，该架构即可泛化，持续挑战并超越已发表基线。

Conclusion: 结构先验效率可作为多书写系统HCR的普适原则：显式编码脚本几何规律作为架构归纳偏置，能同时实现更高精度与更少参数。

Abstract: Non-Latin handwritten character recognition (HCR) remains understudied. Dominant methods consider it as generic image classification, which uses model scale to implicitly learn stroke structure. Structural-prior efficiency---the principle that explicitly encoding script-geometric regularities as architectural inductive biases can be both more accurate and require fewer parameters. We introduce GraphemeNet, a unified multi-script architecture, governed by two orthogonal binary axes. Axis 1 operationalises stroke-level geometric regularity via Persistent Scaffold Injection (PSI): a script-specific asymmetric convolution injects a stroke scaffold as a weighted residual at every encoder stage, continuously anchoring learned features to script geometry---distinct from skip connections, auxiliary losses, or attention reweighting. Axis 2 selects between global average pooling with gated fusion and cross-scale attention with a Stroke Topology Module (STM), depending on whether glyph discrimination requires spatial relational reasoning. A Linear Capsule Routing (LCR) with $O(n)$ routing is shared universally. On fourteen benchmarks across eight writing systems, the architecture generalises with only scaffold and decoder topology varying per script, consistently challenging, outperforming published baselines, and establishing structural-prior efficiency as a broadly applicable principle for multi-script HCR.

</details>


### [81] [AcFlow: Controlling Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers via Learned Conditional Activation Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10723)
*Junran Wang,Zehao Jin,Tianyu Luan,Xinjie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AcFlow 是一种推理时控制器，通过在冻结的 DiT 中，用概念条件速度场搬运中间层图像 token 激活，实现风格强度连续调控与概念抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散 Transformer（DiT）生成能力强，但直接提示对风格强度的控制接口有限，且难以抑制不想要的概念，需要一种可控的干预机制。

Method: AcFlow 在推理阶段保持基础 DiT 冻结，通过学习的“概念条件速度场”对中间层图像 token 激活进行传输；文本概念描述指定干预目标，积分区间提供连续控制参数，速度场产生随 token 变化、依赖激活状态的更新，且同一任务族内跨概念共享参数。

Result: 在风格控制上，AcFlow 在高风格对齐区间取得最佳风格—内容权衡；固定操作点下风格/内容对齐达 0.5365/0.2860，优于风格对齐最高的基线 0.4397/0.2684；定性结果显示可抑制多种概念，包括直接提示失效的情形。

Conclusion: 学习到的速度场可作为一种自适应控制机制，其更新方向随 token 而异并依赖激活状态，无需逐概念拟合即可泛化到未见概念。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion transformers (DiTs) are powerful generators, yet direct prompting provides limited control interface for style intensity and can fail to suppress unwanted concepts. To enable these controls, we introduce AcFlow, an inference-time controller that transports intermediate layer image-token activations through a learned concept-conditioned velocity field while keeping the base DiT frozen. A textual concept description specifies the desired intervention, while the integration horizon provides a continuous control parameter. The field produces token-varying, activation-dependent updates. With parameters shared across concepts within each task family, the field supports fine-grained descriptions and generalizes to concepts unseen during training without per-concept fitting. On style control, AcFlow achieves the best style--content trade-off among the evaluated baselines in the high-style-alignment regime. At a fixed operating point, AcFlow attains style--content alignment of 0.5365/0.2860, compared with 0.4397/0.2684 for the baseline with the highest style alignment. Qualitative results demonstrate suppression of diverse concepts, including cases where direct prompting fails. Our analyses support the learned velocity field as an adaptive control mechanism, with update directions varying across tokens and depend on their activation states. Our code is available at https://github.com/Nove1yst/AcFlow.

</details>


### [82] [MHE-Former: Multi-Hypothesis Transformers via Entropy Maximization for 3D Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10743)
*Boshu Jia,Rongyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Zihao Liu,Yingjie Chen,Zhongqun Zhang,Zhulin Tao,Shaohui Lin,Xiaoyu Wu,Libiao Jin,Baochang Zhang,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"探索-利用"范式解决单目3D手部与身体网格恢复中的遮挡与歧义问题：探索阶段用基于Transformer的多假设框架MHE-Former生成多样且合理的解，利用阶段提出上下文感知的假设选择机制（借助VLM推理与自然语言意图）挑选最优解，在多个数据集上达到精度与多样性的SOTA，并通过用户偏好研究验证其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D手部与身体网格恢复常受严重遮挡和歧义困扰，传统确定性方法只回归单一最优解，导致预测过度自信，无法反映真实存在的不确定性。

Method: 提出探索-利用范式与多假设学习选择框架：探索阶段基于概率建模与熵最大化设计MHE-Former，一个Transformer多假设框架，训练效率高、对标注友好，可生成合理且多样的假设；利用阶段提出假设选择（Hypothesis Selection），这是一个上下文感知的多预测选择流程，特别借助VLM的视觉理解与推理能力，让用户结合额外证据和自然语言意图挑选最合理、最符合需求的估计。

Result: 在多个数据集上，框架在精度和多样性方面均达到SOTA性能；用户偏好研究进一步证明了假设选择流程的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过多假设学习与利用VLM驱动的上下文感知选择，可有效应对遮挡与歧义带来的不确定性，为单目3D网格恢复提供更实用、更符合用户意图的解决方案。

Abstract: Monocular 3D hand and body mesh recovery often suffers from severe occlusion and ambiguity. Traditional deterministic methods typically regress a single optimal solution, leading to overconfident predictions. In this paper, we introduce an exploration--exploitation paradigm for ambiguous mesh recovery with multi-hypothesis learning and selection. Specifically, during exploration, based on our probabilistic formulation and entropy maximization, we propose a novel multi-hypothesis method referred to as MHE-Former. It is a Transformer-based multi-hypothesis framework, ensuring high training efficiency and label friendliness while generating plausible and diverse hypotheses. During exploitation, we propose Hypothesis Selection, a context-aware process for multiple predictions. Especially leveraging VLM's powerful visual understanding and reasoning capabilities, it allows users to choose the most plausible and desired estimate with additional evidence and natural language intent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in accuracy and diversity across multiple datasets. The user preference study further shows the practicality of our hypothesis selection process.

</details>


### [83] [Meta-Learning for Data-Efficient Plant Growth Estimation via Vision Transformers and Fuzzy Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10749)
*Sheikh Hasan Elahi,Rusith Chamara Hathurusinghe Dewage,Habib Ullah,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Rakibul Islam,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出少样本回归框架，结合ViT嵌入、模糊c均值聚类任务构建与梯度元学习，实现温室植物生长估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室监测中植物生长估计需要大量标注数据，而标注获取成本高、耗时长，因此需要在严重标签稀缺条件下实现准确估计。

Method: 利用ViT特征嵌入，基于无标签图像池使用模糊c均值聚类将数据组织为结构化任务，再结合梯度元学习进行少样本回归；系统评估多种元学习方法及簇内支持集选择策略的影响。

Result: 嵌入空间中的任务构建是性能的主要驱动因素；二阶方法（如MAML++等MAML变体）在少样本场景下优于经典基线；簇内支持集选择影响有限且依赖数据集；在两个植物数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化任务设计与元学习相结合，可在严重标签稀缺条件下实现可靠的植物生长估计，任务构建方式比具体元学习算法更为关键。

Abstract: Accurate plant growth estimation is essential for greenhouse monitoring, yet obtaining labeled data remains costly and time-consuming. To address this, we propose a few-shot regression framework that combines Vision Transformer (ViT) feature embeddings, clustering-based task construction, and gradient-based meta-learning, and show that task construction in embedding space is a primary driver of performance. The approach leverages an unlabeled image pool to organize data into structured tasks using fuzzy c-means clustering, enabling efficient learning from a small number of labeled samples. We systematically evaluate meta-learning methods and show that second-order methods (e.g., Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning variants such as MAML++) outperform classical baselines in the few-shot regime. Furthermore, intra-cluster support selection has a limited and dataset-dependent impact. Experiments on two plant datasets show that structured task design combined with meta-learning enables reliable plant growth estimation under severe label scarcity.

</details>


### [84] [GRADE: Single-Frame Generative Radar Depth Estimation Under Visual Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10756)
*Bin Zhao,Patrick Chiou,Nakul Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRADE通过将预训练生成先验与单帧毫米波雷达几何信息结合，在烟雾、雾霾和黑暗等光学传感器失效的环境中实现高保真度量深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学传感器在烟雾、雾霾和黑暗等含悬浮颗粒物的环境中无法穿透，导致密集3D深度感知失效；而毫米波雷达虽在这些条件下仍可正常工作并精确测距，但其小孔径限制了角分辨率。因此需要一种方法在恶劣能见度下仍能获得高保真度量深度。

Method: GRADE首先将原始4D雷达频谱映射为粗略的度量深度；然后使用潜在扩散骨干网络恢复结构细节，并在每个去噪步骤中以该深度估计为条件；此外采用像素空间适配器，在相机线索可用时利用残差相机线索，并在清晰、烟雾退化和遮挡输入上进行训练，使能见度下降时输出逐渐趋近于雷达条件路径。

Result: 在12栋建筑约95K帧含真实烟雾的数据上训练和评估，GRADE在清晰场景下MAE为0.303 m，烟雾条件下为0.313 m，优于现有基线。

Conclusion: GRADE有效结合雷达几何与生成先验，在低能见度条件下实现了高保真度量深度估计，并公开了代码和数据集。

Abstract: Dense 3D depth perception fails under smoke, fog, and darkness because optical sensors cannot penetrate airborne particulates. mmWave radar remains usable and measures range accurately under these conditions, but its small aperture limits angular resolution. We present GRADE, which grounds a pretrained generative prior in single-frame radar geometry to estimate high-fidelity metric depth. GRADE first maps raw 4D radar spectra to coarse metric depth. A latent diffusion backbone then recovers structural detail while conditioning every denoising step on this estimate. A pixel-space adapter uses residual camera cues when available and is trained across clear, smoke-degraded, and occluded inputs so the full output approaches the radar-conditioned path as visibility degrades. Trained and evaluated on ~95K frames across 12 buildings with real smoke, GRADE achieves an MAE of 0.303 m in clear scenes and 0.313 m under smoke, outperforming existing baselines. Code and datasets are available at https://phi-lab-rice.github.io/GRADE.

</details>


### [85] [Shedding Light: A Benchmark for Evaluating Lighting Understanding in Generative Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10787)
*Justine Giroux,Jack Oliver Hilliard,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Jean-François Lalonde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准，通过让生成模型将“光探针”物体以一致的照明条件修复到真实照片中，定量评估生成模型的光照理解与协调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光照的精确建模是真实感图像合成与场景理解的核心，但目前尚不清楚图像生成模型是否真正具备这一能力，物理合理性是否仍是其关键挑战。现有工作在真实感合成上进展显著，但是否真正以物理准确的方式理解光照仍缺乏系统评估。

Method: 利用包含简单物体作为“光探针”的多光照数据集，提示模型将同一物体修复（inpaint）到原图中，并将生成结果与真实光探针对比；随后从修复后的探针中估计光照方向、颜色与辐射度分布，从而量化照明准确性与光度真实感。

Result: 通过从生成的光探针中估计光照方向、颜色和辐射度分布，得到了对生成模型照明准确性与光度真实感的定量度量，并验证了该评估流程可系统刻画模型对真实世界光照的复现能力。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套可扩展的评估协议，用于系统评估生成模型捕捉与复现真实世界光照的能力，为面向任意未来模型的光度准确性基准提供了基础，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of illumination is central to realistic image synthesis and scene understanding. Yet, there is little exploration into whether image generative models are good at this task or whether physical plausibility remains a key challenge for them. Clearly, significant progress has been made in realistic image synthesis, but do models truly understand lighting in a physically accurate manner? To answer this question, this work proposes a benchmark to assess the lighting understanding and harmonisation capabilities of generative models. Our key insight is that evaluating lighting understanding for such models only requires testing how well they insert novel objects into real photographs whilst maintaining consistent illumination. To do so, we use a multi-illumination dataset with images containing simple objects serving as ``light probes'', and prompt models to inpaint the same object onto the original image, then compare the generated results against the ground-truth light probes. We then estimate the lighting direction, colour and radiance distribution from the inpainted probes, providing a quantitative measure of illumination accuracy and photometric realism. Our work establishes a scalable evaluation protocol to systematically assess how well generative models capture and reproduce real-world lighting, offering a foundation for benchmarking the photometric accuracy of any future models. All code and data are available at https://lvsn.github.io/SheddingLight/ .

</details>


### [86] [Two-Parameter Flow Map Learning for Continuous-Time Diffeomorphic Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10789)
*Mohammadjavad Matinkia,Nilanjan Ray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需时间离散化的非自治ODE连续时间解学习框架，通过双参数流映射与余圈一致性约束实现微分同胚图像配准，在九个数据集上提升配准精度并保持微分同胚结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型微分同胚配准多基于自治ODE，通过平稳速度场和scaling-and-squaring恢复形变，表达能力受限；引入时间依赖速度的非自治ODE虽增强表达力，但依赖数值积分隐式约束流结构，使模型表达能力与离散化精度相互纠缠。目标是解耦表达力与离散化精度，直接学习连续时间流映射。

Method: 将非自治ODE的连续时间解建模为双参数流映射，通过强制余圈一致性（时变流的基本结构性质）来学习流映射，训练过程中无需时间离散化和速度积分；推理时仅用少量复合操作即可恢复微分同胚映射，并可无缝集成标准配准骨干网络。

Result: 在九个数据集上一致提升配准精度并保持微分同胚结构：脑MRI基准平均Dice提升2.1%，肺CT的TRE降低12%，心脏MRI与超声数据集Dice提升2.6%。

Conclusion: 通过余圈一致性直接学习非自治ODE的连续时间流映射，可避免训练时的数值积分，兼顾表达力与微分同胚结构，且与现有配准骨干兼容，具有广泛的精度提升效果。

Abstract: Diffeomorphic image registration is central to medical image analysis, enabling anatomically consistent alignment across subjects. Most learning-based diffeomorphic methods model autonomous ODEs(ordinary differential equations) by parameterizing a stationary velocity field and recovering deformations via scaling-and-squaring. While non-autonomous ODEs with time-dependent velocities increase expressiveness, existing approaches rely on numerical integration to implicitly enforce flow structure that entangles model expressiveness with discretization accuracy. We propose a framework to directly learn the continuous-time solution of a non-autonomous ODE formulated as a two-parameterflow map. By enforcing cocycle consistency, a fundamental structural property of time-varying flows, we learn the flow maps without time discretization and velocity integration during training. The framework recovers diffeomorphic mappings at inference using a small number of compositions. Our proposed framework seamlessly incorporates standard registration backbones and improves alignment accuracy consistently across nine datasets while preserving diffeomorphic structure. Notably, the proposed method achieves an average Dice improvement of 2.1% on brain MRI benchmarks, a 12% TRE reduction on lung CT, and a 2.6% Dice gain on cardiac MRI and ultrasound datasets.

</details>


### [87] [How Much Velocity Does Off-Ball Space Value Need? A Broadcast-Viewport Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10801)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了广播视角下无球分析中速度信息在哪一层产生作用：速度对插补几乎无用，对控制面误差有一阶贡献（约11–15%），对球队判定的贡献则小一个数量级；真正起作用的是"可见通道"的速度，遮挡球员速度的完美估计仅增益2–6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播视角下半个球场球员在画面外且画面内速度受标定漂移影响，速度感知的控球分析在"插补、控制面、球队判定"三层中究竟哪一层依赖速度尚不清楚，需要厘清速度信息的价值层级，并给出不同景别（视口宽度）下的取舍策略。

Method: 沿用已有的画面外插补协议（三场 Metrica 数据、44 m 视口、block-bootstrap 置信区间），在三个分析层（插补、控制面、球队判定）上比较四种速度方案（无速度、视口内可观测速度、可见球员真实速度、全体真实速度），并对照速度感知的 ground truth；进一步用十一档噪声/抖动设置（含与 sigma_v 匹配的对照）构建噪声梯度，并用 SoccerNet-GSR 单场十一段视频经由同一流程测量 sigma_v=1.65 m/s 的表现、方差结构与重尾聚类特性。

Result: 插补层速度几乎无用（相对无速度面 MAE 12–14 pp 仅 -0.2 pp）；控制面层为一阶效应（-1.5 至 -1.8 pp，占该 MAE 的 11–15%）；球队判定层小十倍（-0.12 至 -0.19 pp）。起作用的是可见通道：遮挡球员的完美速度仅增加 2–6% 的可见增益，无任何 last-seen decay 策略更优。省略速度使控制面变模糊（逐帧 |e| 2.2–2.6 pp）但时间平均偏差小（每格 ≤0.4 pp），而插补误差是偏向防守方深区的结构化偏差（5–9 pp）。噪声梯度主要由单一速度噪声轴 sigma_v 一阶排序，盈亏平衡约 1 m/s；实测 sigma_v=1.65 m/s 仍能恢复 24–36% 的收益，其中 43% 方差为帧间共模（控制面可容忍），残差重尾且聚集，与高斯对照（+0.03 vs. +0.36）不一致。速度可消除的无速度误差占比随视口宽度增大：36 m 时 7%，60 m 时 21%。

Conclusion: 速度信息在广播无球分析中的价值高度分层：应把精力放在插补精度上应对紧景别（窄视口），而在宽景别下才值得引入速度建模；由于收益主要来自可见球员的观测速度，遮挡球员的速度恢复或复杂衰减策略收益有限，且实测噪声的共模与重尾结构意味着高斯假设的灵敏度分析会误判收益。

Abstract: Velocity-aware pitch control is standard, but under a broadcast viewport half the players are off screen and on-screen velocities come from a drifting calibration. We ask at which layer of broadcast off-ball analysis velocity changes the answer. Inheriting our off-screen imputation protocol (three Metrica matches, 44 m viewport, block-bootstrap CIs), we score four velocity regimes -- none, viewport-legal observed, true-for-visible, true-for-all -- against a velocity-aware ground truth at three layers: imputation, the control surface, and team verdicts. Velocity is nearly useless for imputation (-0.2 pp against a 12--14 pp velocity-free surface MAE), first-order for the surface (-1.5 to -1.8 pp, 11--15% of that MAE), and ten times smaller for verdicts (-0.12 to -0.19 pp). The velocity that matters is the visible channel: perfect occluded-player velocity adds 2--6% of the visible gain, and no last-seen decay policy we tested exceeds that. Omitting velocity blurs the surface (per-frame |e| 2.2--2.6 pp) with small time-averaged bias (per cell <=0.4 pp), whereas imputation error is a structured bias against the defending team's deep zone (5--9 pp). At a fixed velocity window, a noise ladder of eleven jitter settings, including sigma_v-matched pairs, is ordered to first order by one velocity-noise axis sigma_v with break-even ~1 m/s; eleven SoccerNet-GSR clips from one match through our pipeline measure sigma_v=1.65 m/s yet recover 24--36% of the benefit: 43% of the variance is frame-common, which the surface tolerates, and the residual is heavy-tailed and clustered, which Gaussian controls matched on component RMS do not reproduce (+0.03 vs. +0.36). The share of velocity-free error that velocity removes grows with viewport width (7% at 36 m, 21% at 60 m): fix imputation on tight shots, velocity on wide ones. Code and logs are released.

</details>


### [88] [TrajFusionNet+: Transformer-Based Prediction of Pedestrian Crossing Intention via Fusion of Trajectory Representations and Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10806)
*François G. Landry,Moulay A. Akhloufi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajFusionNet+，一种基于Transformer的行人过街意图预测模型，融合序列、视觉与图三种轨迹表示，在PIE和JAAD数据集上取得新的最优性能，并在跨数据集联合训练评估协议下展现出更强的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人过街意图预测对自动驾驶安全至关重要。现有方法多依赖单一模态表示，难以同时刻画行人轨迹的时序动态、视觉外观线索以及场景中行人与交通要素的关系依赖，且模型跨数据集泛化能力不足，因此需要多模态融合且泛化性更强的模型。

Method: 提出TrajFusionNet+，在原有TrajFusionNet基础上扩展为三分支架构：序列注意力模块（SAM）处理过去与预测轨迹的序列表示；视觉注意力模块（VAM）将观测与预测边界框叠加到场景图像上形成视觉表示；图注意力模块（GAM）从分割场景图像中提取以行人为中心的图，捕捉行人与交通要素间的依赖关系。三者融合进行过街意图预测。

Result: 在PIE和JAAD这两个最常用的行人过街意图数据集上达到改进的最优性能。此外在新评估协议下（PIE与JAAD联合训练、各自单独测试），TrajFusionNet+相比现有方法展现出更优的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多模态（序列、视觉、图）融合与注意力机制能有效提升行人过街意图预测性能，且联合训练评估协议有助于衡量并提升模型跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: The pedestrian crossing intention task involves predicting whether pedestrians are likely to cross the road from the point of view of an autonomous vehicle. We introduce TrajFusionNet+, a novel transformer-based model for pedestrian crossing intention prediction. TrajFusionNet+ combines sequential and visual representations of pedestrian trajectory with a graph-based representation of the scene context in order to predict pedestrian crossing intention. The proposed architecture builds upon our previous model, TrajFusionNet, and comprises three branches: a Sequence Attention Module (SAM), which processes a sequential representation of past and predicted pedestrian trajectories; a Visual Attention Module (VAM), which utilizes a visual representation of the pedestrian trajectories by overlaying observed and predicted bounding boxes onto scene images; and a Graph Attention Module (GAM), which extracts pedestrian-centric graphs from segmented scene images and captures the relational dependencies between pedestrians and traffic elements. TrajFusionNet+ achieves improved state-of-the-art performance on the two most widely used pedestrian crossing intention datasets, PIE and JAAD. Furthermore, we introduce a new evaluation protocol in which models are trained jointly on the PIE and JAAD datasets but evaluated separately on each. Under this setting, TrajFusionNet+ demonstrates superior generalization compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [89] [Overpainting: Localized Context-aware Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10811)
*Sam Sartor,Iliyan Georgiev,Michael Fischer,Valentin Deschaintre,Pieter Peers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"overpainting"图像编辑操作，用trimap(白/灰/黑)控制编辑区域及对原内容的感知，通过改造预训练扩散模型并配合自动化数据生成流程实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑需兼顾编辑位置控制与对原区域内容的感知，同时支持精确与宽松两种控制程度以满足不同用户意图。

Method: 以trimap定义可编辑性（白必改、灰可改、黑不可改）；通过联合注意力(joint attention)与低秩适配(low-rank adaptation, LoRA)改造预训练扩散编辑模型，并采用attention-dropout平衡噪声、源图和掩码间的信息流；提出自动化数据生成流程：用语言驱动编辑模型生成候选图像对、筛选、并提取trimap。

Result: 在多种编辑任务上展示了overpainting模型的多功能性。

Conclusion: overpainting提供了一种兼具位置控制与原内容感知、支持精确与宽松控制的图像编辑范式，结合自动化数据构建流程可广泛适用。

Abstract: We present "overpainting", an image editing operation which offers both control over the location of the edit and awareness of the previous content in that location. The overpainted area is given by a trimap, where white-annotated pixels must be edited, gray-annotated pixels may be edited, and black-annotated pixels must not be edited. This enables both precise and loose control, depending on user intent.
  We implement overpainting by adapting a pretrained image editing diffusion model using a combination of joint attention and low-rank adaption across input images with attention-dropout to balance the information flow between noise, source and mask images. We present a novel, automated, training data generation pipeline that (1) generates a set of candidate image pairs leveraging existing language-based editing models, (2) carefully curates those pairs, and (3) extracts a trimap from each usable pair. We demonstrate the versatility of our overpainting model on a wide range of editing tasks.

</details>


### [90] [BodyCam-VQA: Enhanced Body-Worn Camera Video Captioning via Multimodal Reasoning and Probe Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10815)
*Karish Gupta,Matthew Alex,Alex Li,Yang Wu,Yun-Wei Chu,Kashif Munir,Xiaotian Zhou,Zhengping Ji,Xiaozhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向执法场景的自适应视觉问答（VQA）框架，通过结构化推理从警用随身摄像头（BWC）视频中提取细粒度视觉证据，以克服现有视觉语言模型在低质量、高噪声执法视频中漏检关键取证细节的问题，从而生成更可靠、客观、详细的执法记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警用随身摄像头（BWC）视频对执法透明、警员问责与公民权利保护至关重要，但其多模态视频格式带来处理难题：画面混乱、视觉质量低、运动与交互快速、音频噪声大，即便是当前最先进的视觉语言模型也难以有效理解。现有VLM常忽略关键取证细节，如有价值证据的存在、嫌疑人与警员交互中的潜在细微差别，而这些信息对公正司法结果及警民安全至关重要。因此需要一种针对高风险执法场景的专用方法。

Method: 作者提出一种面向高风险执法场景的自适应视觉问答（VQA）框架，采用结构化推理（structured reasoning）方式来抽取传统描述（captioning）系统无法捕捉的细粒度视觉证据。研究中对比实验了多种问题生成模型，包括基础模型（foundation models）与经过微调的开源权重模型（fine-tuned open-weight models），以考察不同问题生成模型实现所带来的性能差异。

Result: 实验结果表明，该VQA驱动架构能够为执法事件提供更可靠、更客观、更详细的记录；同时发现不同问题生成模型实现之间存在性能差异（涵盖基础模型与微调开源权重模型）。

Conclusion: 该自适应VQA框架通过AI辅助的取证清晰度（AI-assisted forensic clarity），成为保护执法人员与公众的有力工具，提升了执法记录的客观性与细节完整度。

Abstract: Police body-worn camera (BWC) footage has emerged as a critical aspect of law enforcement that ensures legal transparency, officer accountability, and the protection of civil rights. However, effectively processing this data remains a significant challenge due to its multimodal video format. BWC videos, in many cases, comprise chaotic scenes with low visual quality, rapid movement/interactions, and high-noise audio that make visual understanding a challenge for even SOTA multimodal models. Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently overlook critical forensic details, such as the presence of valuable evidence or the latent nuances of suspect-officer interactions, which are vital for fair legal outcomes and civilian/officer safety. To address these limitations, we propose an Adaptive Visual Question Answering (VQA) framework engineered for high-stakes law enforcement. Our framework employs a structured reasoning approach to extract fine-grained visual evidence that traditional captioning systems fail to capture. We experiment with multiple question generation models, including foundation models and fine-tuned open-weight models, to observe performance variation among question generation model implementations. Our results demonstrate that this VQA-driven architecture provides a more reliable, objective, and detailed record of enforcement events, ultimately serving as a powerful tool to protect both law enforcement officers and the public through AI-assisted forensic clarity.

</details>


### [91] [Are We Really Doing Few-Shot Learning? A Critical Examination of Pre-Training Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10851)
*Alejandro Galan-Cuenca,Marcelo Saval-Calvo,Antonio Javier Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了无预训练、类不交同域预训练、有监督跨域预训练与无标签跨域预训练在少样本学习中的效果，指出类不交并不足以消除目标域信息影响，同域预训练会带来约9.66个百分点的乐观偏差，并提出可估计源域适配性的源选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本学习通常在大规模辅助集上预训练，且要求辅助集与目标episode类别不交但同域。作者质疑这种协议是否真正反映低数据学习，担心同域数据的存在会高估真实场景（目标域数据稀缺）下的性能。

Method: 在8个数据集、3种少样本架构和多种way-shot设置下，对比四类设置：无预训练、类不交同域预训练、有监督跨域预训练、无标签跨域预训练；并考察基于增强的无标签跨域策略以及基于描述符的源域选择策略（预训练前估计源域适配性）。

Result: 同域预训练平均提升33.41个百分点，有监督跨域预训练提升23.75个百分点，域重叠带来9.66个百分点的乐观偏差；跨域预训练效果强依赖源域与目标域兼容性；无标签增强策略平均增益27.71个百分点，接近有监督跨域预训练的27.97个百分点；源选择策略与oracle选择的中位差距仅1.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅保证类不交不足以消除目标域数据影响，同域预训练作为默认少样本评测协议会高估目标域数据稀缺时的真实性能，应转向更现实的跨域或标签无关预训练设置，并辅以源域选择。

Abstract: Few-shot learning is commonly evaluated under protocols that pre-train a model on a large auxiliary set whose classes are disjoint from the target episodes yet drawn from the same visual domain. This paper examines whether such protocols truly reflect low-data learning. We systematically compare no pre-training, class-disjoint in-domain pre-training, supervised out-of-domain pre-training, and label-free out-of-domain pre-training across eight datasets, three few-shot architectures, and multiple way-shot settings. Our results show that class disjointness alone is insufficient to remove the influence of target-domain data. In-domain pre-training improves over no pre-training by 33.41 percentage points on average, whereas supervised out-of-domain pre-training yields 23.75 percentage points, revealing a 9.66-point optimistic bias associated with domain overlap. Although out-of-domain pre-training is more realistic in applications where target-domain data are scarce, its effectiveness depends strongly on the compatibility between source and target domains. We further show that labeled source data are not strictly required, with an augmentation-based label-free strategy reaching an average gain of 27.71 percentage points and closely matching supervised out-of-domain pre-training at 27.97 percentage points. Finally, we introduce a descriptor-based source-selection strategy that estimates source-domain suitability before pre-training, reaching a median gap of only 1.37 percentage points to oracle selection. These findings highlight the need to move beyond in-domain pre-training as the default few-shot evaluation protocol, since it can overestimate performance in realistic scenarios where target-domain data are scarce.

</details>


### [92] [Evaluation of Vision-Language Models Across Diverse Coastal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10855)
*Seth Knoop,Chad R. Samuelson,Gabriel R. Slade,Brady Moon,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了夏威夷瓦胡岛沿海密集标注数据集（1000+图像、18类、7400+实例），通过文本-掩码、掩码-掩码、掩码-文本三类实验评测7个视觉语言模型，发现沿海概念识别最难，但其性能劣势主要源于分割与语言表征而非环境语境本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）被广泛用于机器人感知，但它们在沿海环境中的表现尚缺乏系统评估。作者希望填补这一空白，检验沿海场景是否本质上比陆地场景更难，还是性能差异源于其他因素。

Method: 构建密集标注的沿海数据集：来自夏威夷瓦胡岛三个区域、七次任务的1000余张图像，涵盖18个语义类别、超过7400个标注实例。对7个现代VLM开展三类互补实验：文本到掩码、掩码到掩码、掩码到文本对齐评测，并对比沿海与陆地数据集中的共享常规类别，考察替代文本标签的影响。

Result: 景观大类识别普遍优于常规物体类和沿海类，沿海概念最具挑战性。但对比沿海与陆地数据集中的共享常规类，未发现仅由环境语境导致的稳定性能差异；掩码到掩码匹配在常规类和沿海类之间表现相近；使用替代文本标签可显著提升若干沿海概念的识别效果。

Conclusion: 沿海类别（至少在所评测的物体/查询类别上）性能偏低，很大程度上受分割质量和语言表征影响，而非沿海环境本身所致。这提示改进方向应聚焦于分割精度与文本标签/语义表征设计，而非单纯归因于环境语境。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable robotic per- ception by associating visual observations with natural-language concepts. Yet their performance in coastal environments remains largely unexplored. We introduce a densely labeled coastal dataset containing more than 1,000 images collected across seven missions in three regions of Oahu, Hawaii, with 18 semantic classes and over 7,400 annotated instances. We evaluate seven modern VLMs through three complementary experiments mea- suring text-to-mask, mask-to-mask, and mask-to-text alignment. Broad landscape classes are generally recognized more accurately than conventional object and coastal classes, with coastal con- cepts presenting the greatest challenge. However, comparisons of shared conventional classes across coastal and terrestrial datasets reveal no consistent performance difference attributable solely to environmental context. Mask-to-mask matching also remains similar across conventional and coastal classes, while alternative textual labels substantially improve recognition of several coastal concepts. These results suggest that lower performance on coastal classes (at least on the objects/query categories evaluated) is heavily influenced by segmentation and linguistic representation.

</details>


### [93] [Symmetry-aware super-resolution of crystal orientation maps via invariant latent-space learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10898)
*Umang Garg,Warren Zamudio,McLean P. Echlin,Samantha H. Daly,Tresa M. Pollock,B. S. Manjunath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SG-SRAN，一种晶体对称群感知的超分辨率注意力网络，在SO(3)/G商空间中进行EBSD取向图超分辨率，仅用27-49k可训练参数即匹配15-16M参数主干网络，并保持最低p68误差与高IPF保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD取向图本质上是仅定义到晶体对称性之下的物理场，数据位于商空间SO(3)/G之上。常规欧氏图像超分辨率方法会混合对称等价的取向表示，导致晶界模糊；同时EBSD采集时间限制其空间分辨率，因此需要一种在设计中即融入晶体对称性与晶界保持的超分辨率方法。

Method: 1) 采用冻结的局部等距编码器，将等价取向映射到统一隐表示，使该空间中欧氏距离近似于取向差（misorientation）。2) 在该隐空间中执行超分辨率，每个高分辨率token被限制在特征一致的局部支撑域内，防止跨晶界混合。3) 使用基于字典的解码器恢复有效取向。

Result: 在FCC与HCP基准上，SG-SRAN仅用27-49k可训练参数即匹配15-16M参数的主干网络，取得最低的p68误差、最高的反极图（IPF）保真度，并能零样本迁移到未见合金。

Conclusion: 将晶体对称性作为网络设计的先验、在商空间SO(3)/G中进行超分辨率，可在极小参数量下实现高精度、晶界保持的EBSD取向图超分辨率，并具备跨合金泛化能力。

Abstract: Crystal-orientation maps are physical fields defined only up to crystal symmetry; electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) resolves them experimentally, but acquisition-time constraints limit spatial resolution. Unlike conventional images, EBSD data lie on the quotient space $\mathrm{SO}(3)/G$, where $G$ is the crystal-symmetry group. Standard Euclidean interpolation can therefore mix symmetry-equivalent representations and blur grain boundaries. We introduce the Symmetry-Group-Aware Super-Resolution Attention Network (SG-SRAN), which incorporates crystal symmetry and boundary preservation by design. A frozen, locally isometric encoder maps equivalent orientations to a common latent representation in which Euclidean distance approximates misorientation. Super-resolution is performed in this space, with each high-resolution token restricted to a feature-consistent local support to prevent cross-boundary mixing. A dictionary-based decoder then recovers valid orientations. Across FCC and HCP benchmarks, SG-SRAN matches 15-16 million parameter backbones using only 27-49k trainable parameters, while achieving the lowest p68 errors, highest inverse-pole-figure fidelity, and zero-shot transfer to unseen alloys.

</details>


### [94] [HiPerViT: A Hierarchical Perceiver-Vision Transformer Architecture for Multi-Scale Texture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10917)
*João Pedro C. A. de Sá,Odemir Martinez Bruno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPerViT 是一种纯视觉架构，通过将二阶统计先验显式注入 Transformer 识别流程，提升纹理识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉模型在纹理识别上面临挑战，因为判别性证据常来自高阶空间统计而非物体形状。Vision Transformer 虽具强长程建模能力，但其标准的以物体为中心的表征未显式暴露此类统计结构，限制了细粒度识别中的纹理敏感性。

Method: 提出 HiPerViT：结合全局与局部图像视图，将紧凑双线性描述子编码为统计 token，并通过 Perceiver 式潜在蒸馏与一阶空间表征整合，使空间 token 与二阶特征共现统计直接交互。无需多模态预训练或集成构建。

Result: 在六个纹理识别基准上，HiPerViT 相较强纯视觉基线均取得一致提升，包括 DTD +3.05、GTOS-Mobile +10.48、1200Tex +10.10 个百分点。

Conclusion: 增益对提取二阶统计的主干深度及交互与蒸馏阶段的顺序基本不变，说明提升主要来源并非特定融合拓扑，而是二阶统计信息作为一等表征信号的显式可用性。显式统计 token 化是纹理中心视觉识别的有效且稳健的设计原则。

Abstract: Texture recognition remains challenging for modern vision models because discriminative evidence is often carried by higher-order spatial statistics rather than by object shape alone. While Vision Transformers provide strong long-range modeling capacity, their standard object-centric representations do not explicitly expose such statistical structure, which limits texture sensitivity in fine-grained recognition settings. We present HiPerViT, a compact vision-only architecture that injects an explicit second-order statistical prior into a transformer-based recognition pipeline. The method combines global and local image views with a compact bilinear descriptor encoded as a statistical token, and integrates this token with first-order spatial representations through Perceiver-style latent distillation. This design enables direct interaction between spatial tokens and second-order feature co-occurrence statistics, providing the model with explicit access to texture-relevant information without requiring multimodal pretraining or ensemble construction. Across six texture recognition benchmarks, HiPerViT achieves consistent improvements over strong vision-only baselines under the reported evaluation protocols, including gains of +3.05 percentage points on DTD, +10.48 on GTOS-Mobile, and +10.10 on 1200Tex. Beyond benchmark performance, our analyses show that these gains are largely invariant to the backbone depth used to extract second-order statistics and to the ordering of interaction and distillation stages. This pattern suggests that the primary source of improvement is not a specific fusion topology, but the explicit availability of second-order statistical information as a first-class representational signal. These results support explicit statistical tokenization as an effective and robust design principle for texture-centric visual recognition.

</details>


### [95] [CamPilot: A Multi-Agent Cinematic Assistant for Camera-Controlled Movie Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10943)
*Yang Wu,Stefano Petrangeli,Ishita Dasgupta,Yu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CamPilot：基于多智能体与GRPO学习的电影运镜规划框架，配合CamEval基准，提升文本到电影生成的运镜控制与电影化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的视频生成在专业电影制作层面存在不足：电影化语言（运镜）不够精细，缺乏人类摄影设计感，且多镜头连续性难以保证。

Method: 提出CamPilot多智能体框架，整合电影化规划与运镜控制；采用GRPO学习范式，从14K真实专业电影中学习运镜规划，内化运动模式与构图原则，支持对拍摄技法（机位角度、运动、焦距行为）及跨镜头关系的推理；多智能体协作与演化以提升整体质量；并构建CamEval基准评估运镜质量与电影化吸引力。

Result: 实验表明，CamPilot在运镜控制与质量上优于当前最先进的文本到电影生成方法。

Conclusion: 专业级运镜设计对电影生成效果具有显著影响，CamPilot验证了将电影化规划与多智能体协作引入视频生成的有效性，并为该领域后续研究提供了基准支持。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into video generation has enabled rapid text-to-video creation and improved visual quality. However, it still falls short of professional filmmaking, where cinematographic language is less refined than human-crafted camera work and multi-shot continuity remains challenging. To address these limitations, we introduce CamPilot, a multi-agent framework that integrates cinematographic planning and camera-work control to produce more coherent, logically structured, and human-aesthetic movies. CamPilot adopts a GRPO-based learning paradigm to learn camera work planning from 14K real-world professional movies, internalizing motion patterns and composition principles that support reasoning over shooting techniques (e.g., camera angle, motion, and focal behavior) and cross-shot relationships for controllable camera-viewpoint generation. Multiple agents further collaborate and evolve to improve overall output quality. To support this work and further studies in this domain, we establish CamEval, a benchmark for evaluating camera work quality and cinematic engagement. Empirical results show that CamPilot outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-movie generation methods on cinematographic control and quality, highlighting the impact of professional camera design on movie generation.

</details>


### [96] [New Evidence, Same Choice: Testing Physical Experiment Selection in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11022)
*Sourajit Saha,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Nobin Sarwar,Shaswati Saha,Yuxuan Jiang,Siyuan Li,Qiheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出受控评测基准，检验视觉语言模型能否决定何时直接作答、何时选择最便宜的补充实验；结果显示模型在物理证据选择上存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理推理基准通常只评估最终答案，无法直接衡量模型“何时作答、作答还需何种实验”的决策能力。作者希望将证据选择与最终答案评估分离，揭示传统答案准确率掩盖的物理推理局限。

Method: 设计受控任务：每个问题提供一张测量图像，并由两种质量与另一种相关属性的两取值组合成四个可能物理世界。模型须选择停止作答，或选择能解决该问题的最便宜补充实验。构建配对问题，其中改变观测测量或改变问题都会改变最优动作。由于所有可能世界与实验成本已知，可显式确定最优选择。评测覆盖六个开源模型与144个物理参数集。

Result: 直接回答时，模型对95.1%至100%的图像对重复同一动作，即使正确动作已改变。简要推理可改善动作切换，但最佳模型仅对5.9%的图像对同时做出两个正确决策。进一步分析揭示测量解读、物理推理与响应格式方面的失败。

Conclusion: 通过将证据选择与最终答案分开评估，该基准暴露了常规答案准确率容易忽视的物理推理局限，表明视觉语言模型在主动实验决策上仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: A model first sees an image from one physical measurement experiment, such as how far a block coasted, and must answer a question about a new trial, such as whether the block will pass a target after a fixed push. The initial experiment may provide enough information to answer, or the model may need another measurement, such as the object's mass, friction, restitution, or spring stiffness. We study whether vision language models can decide when to answer immediately and, when more evidence is needed, which experiment to perform. Current physical reasoning benchmarks usually evaluate only the final answer, so they do not directly measure this decision-making ability. We introduce a controlled evaluation where each problem provides one measurement image and four possible physical worlds created by combining two possible masses and two possible values of another relevant property. The model must either stop and answer or select the cheapest additional experiment that can resolve the question. We construct matched problem pairs where changing either the observed measurement or the question changes the optimal action. Since all possible worlds and experiment costs are known, we can explicitly determine the optimal choice. Across six open models and 144 physical parameter sets, direct responses repeat the same action for 95.1% to 100% of image pairs even when the correct action changes. Brief reasoning improves action switching, but the best model makes both decisions correctly for only 5.9% of image pairs. Additional analysis reveals failures in measurement interpretation, physical reasoning, and response formatting. By evaluating evidence selection separately from final answers, our benchmark reveals limitations in physical reasoning that conventional answer accuracy can overlook.

</details>


### [97] [Toward Interpretable Multimodal Fusion: Heat Conduction Modeling for Hyperspectral and LiDAR Joint Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11040)
*Kan Wei,Jiahui Cui,Jing Yao,Xinyu Zhao,Lei Wang,Pedram Ghamisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出物理启发框架M2Heat，借鉴热传导原理实现高光谱与LiDAR数据的多模态融合，通过vHeat模块、增强频率值嵌入和Cross-Frequency Fusion模块，以亚二次复杂度捕获全局依赖，在Trento、Houston2013和Augsburg三个基准上取得有竞争力的分类性能，并提供可解释的融合视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱与LiDAR数据融合对土地覆盖分类至关重要，可联合利用光谱、空间和结构信息。但现有多模态融合方法难以在保持计算效率的同时建模长程依赖和复杂的各向异性交互。

Method: 提出M2Heat物理启发框架，将多模态融合类比为热传导过程。核心包括物理驱动的视觉热传导模块（vHeat）和增强频率值嵌入（FVEs），模拟各向异性信息流，以亚二次复杂度捕获全局依赖；并结合混合空间-频率融合策略Cross-Frequency Fusion（CFF）模块生成判别性强、鲁棒的特征表示。

Result: 在Trento、Houston2013和Augsburg三个基准数据集上取得有竞争力的整体性能，同时提供可解释的热传导引导的多模态特征融合视角。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 结果表明热传导引导的神经算子在高光谱-LiDAR多模态融合中具有实现高效、可解释遥感触觉融合的潜力。

Abstract: The fusion of hyperspectral (HS) and Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) data plays a crucial role in enhancing land-cover classification by jointly exploiting spectral, spatial, and structural cues. However, existing multimodal fusion methods still struggle to model long-range dependencies and complex anisotropic interactions while maintaining computational efficiency. This paper introduces M2Heat, a physics-inspired framework that investigates multimodal fusion through the lens of heat conduction. At its core, a physics-driven visual heat conduction module (vHeat) and enhanced Frequency Value Embeddings (FVEs) simulate anisotropic information flow, enabling the capture of global dependencies with sub-quadratic complexity and physical interpretability. This mechanism, combined with a hybrid spatial-frequency fusion strategy named Cross-Frequency Fusion (CFF) module, produces highly discriminative and robust feature representations. M2Heat achieves competitive overall performance on three benchmarks, i.e., Trento, Houston2013, and Augsburg, while providing an interpretable heat-conduction-guided perspective for multimodal feature fusion. These results indicate the potential of heat-conduction-guided neural operators for efficient and interpretable RS multimodal fusion. The source code is publicly available at https: /github.com/Weikan0425/M2Heat_HSI_LiDAR.

</details>


### [98] [Meta-Learning for Classifier Selection in Image Datasets: A Feature-Driven Framework for Accuracy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11041)
*Zahra Nabizadeh_Shahre_Babak,Farzaneh Koohestani,Nader Karimi,Shahram Shirani,Shadrokh Samavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于元学习的框架，利用刻画数据集复杂度的元特征预测分类器性能，在56个图像数据集上平均排序预测准确率超过86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No Free Lunch定理表明不存在普适最优分类器，而为特定图像数据集选择合适的分类器因图像本身复杂多样而困难；穷举训练所有模型以比较性能的计算成本过高，因此需要一种无需完整训练即可预测分类器表现的方法。

Method: 构建元学习框架：提取并筛选能刻画数据集复杂度的元特征（使用自编码器、预训练网络、降维技术等），训练回归模型估计分类器准确率；同时采用聚类方法将性能模式相似的分类器分组，以简化推荐流程。数据集涵盖自然、动物、数字、摩托车、医学图像、人体等多种概念。

Result: 在56个多样化图像数据集上评估，所提方法的平均排序预测准确率超过86%，能有效指导模型选择。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展且具可解释性，为在降低计算成本的同时提升分类性能提供了实用方案。

Abstract: No Free Lunch theorem implies that any performance gains achieved by a classifier on a particular image distribution are necessarily offset by a loss of performance over the set of all possible problems; thus, no single model is universally optimal. Selecting the most suitable classifier for image datasets is a critical yet challenging task due to the intrinsic complexity and diversity of images. This paper proposes a meta-learning framework that leverages a comprehensive set of meta-features capturing dataset complexity to predict classifier performance without exhaustive training. By extracting and selecting features using methods such as autoencoders, pre-trained networks, and dimensionality reduction techniques, we train regression models to efficiently estimate classifier accuracies. Additionally, clustering techniques are employed to group classifiers with similar performance patterns, simplifying the recommendation process. The datasets used span a wide range of concepts, including nature, animals, numbers, motorcycles, medical images, and human bodies, to ensure broad generalization. Evaluated on 56 diverse image datasets, our approach achieves an average ranking prediction accuracy exceeding 86%, demonstrating its effectiveness in guiding model selection. This scalable and interpretable framework provides a practical solution to improve classification performance while reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [99] [TailProp: content-adaptive light- and heavy-tailed propagation for vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11081)
*Jiahao Kong,Zihan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TailProp层次化视觉骨干网络，基于混合高斯与柯西稳定过程传播子的Tail Propagation Operator，实现跨机制自适应传播，在分类、检测、分割等任务上一致超越匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学启发的视觉模型虽能以显式传播动力学替代传统token混合，但通常在单一动力学族内构造并调整视觉传播，而视觉表示在不同样本、通道和网络阶段往往需要差异较大的空间交互方式，因此需要探索跨机制的自适应传播。

Method: 以高斯与柯西稳定过程传播子作为互补基，分别提供快速衰减与重尾的空间影响；预测内容条件化的通道级系数自适应组合二者；由于该系数在空间上共享，两种响应可直接在DCT域融合，仅需一对DCT/IDCT，在固定通道宽度下对方形特征图实现O(N^1.5)（N=HW）的空间混合；并据此构建层次化骨干TailProp。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、语义分割、鲁棒性和跨骨干恢复任务上均一致优于匹配的传播基线；TailProp-B在ImageNet-1K达84.4% Top-1，在3x Mask R-CNN下为50.3/44.8 box/mask AP，ADE20K为50.8% mIoU。消融实验表明增益不能归因于单基传播、额外同族分支或族内自适应阶数。

Conclusion: 互补双基传播是视觉表示学习的一种有效设计原则，跨机制自适应传播相比单一动力学族更具优势，且TailProp在效率与性能上兼具竞争力。

Abstract: Science-inspired vision models show that explicit propagation dynamics can provide structured and interpretable alternatives to conventional token mixing. Existing formulations, however, typically construct and adapt visual propagation within a particular dynamical family, while visual representations can require substantially different spatial interactions across samples, channels, and network stages. We explore cross-regime adaptive propagation and introduce TailProp, a hierarchical vision backbone built upon the Tail Propagation Operator (TPO). TPO uses Gaussian and Cauchy stable-process propagators as complementary bases with rapidly decaying and heavy-tailed spatial influence, and predicts a content-conditioned channel-wise coefficient to adaptively combine them. Because this coefficient is spatially shared, the two responses are fused directly in the DCT domain with a single DCT/IDCT pair, yielding $O(N^{1.5})$ spatial mixing for square feature maps with $N=HW$ and fixed channel width. Across image classification, object detection, semantic segmentation, robustness, and cross-backbone restoration, TailProp consistently outperforms matched propagation baselines; TailProp-B reaches 84.4% Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K, 50.3/44.8 box/mask AP under the 3x Mask R-CNN schedule, and 50.8% mIoU on ADE20K. Controlled ablations further show that these gains are not explained by single-basis propagation, an additional same-family branch, or within-family adaptive order alone, supporting complementary two-basis propagation as an effective design principle for visual representation learning.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Benchmarks: Using VLMs to Reveal Systematic Classification Failures Under Real World Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11126)
*Dieuwertje Alblas,Alma M. Liezenga,Jan Erik van Woerden,Fedor Taggenbrock,Dalia Aljawaheri,Klamer Schutte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文初步评估了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的错误切片检测（ESD）方法在国防领域分类模型验证与确认（V&V）中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类模型的V&V通常依赖耗时的人工检查来发现错误样本中的系统性模式，效率低下。将VLM引入该流程有望加速V&V，但国防领域面临两大挑战：(1) 国防数据在VLM训练语料中代表性不足；(2) 国防场景的环境与上下文多样性较低。

Method: 提出一种基于VLM的错误切片检测（ESD）方法，利用VLM将图像嵌入到具有语义意义的向量空间中，并对分类模型的系统性错误进行独立分组与标注。

Result: 在非军事数据集上，该方法能识别出具有实际运行相关性的、人工添加的扰动；在军事场景下，方法可基于图像环境进行聚类与描述，但聚类描述之间存在重叠。作者还进一步比较了两个数据集在嵌入变异度上的差异。

Conclusion: 结果尚不足以支持完全自动化的VLM-based ESD V&V流程，但表明VLM未来可用于加速V&V过程。

Abstract: Verification and validation (V&V) of classification models is crucial to enable a wide range of sensor processing applications. Currently, the V&V process relies on time-consuming manual inspection of erroneous samples to find meaningful patterns. This work explores the use of Vision Language Models (VLMs) to speed up this laborious process. VLMs are trained to embed images into a semantically meaningful vector representation, from which human-interpretable systematic errors can be distilled. Deploying such VLM-based methods in a defence context introduces two major challenges: (1) the defence domain is underrepresented in the training data of VLMs, and (2) surroundings and context are less diverse than for other domains. This study provides an initial assessment of the suitability of VLM-based methods for V&V of defence applications. We propose a VLM-based error slice detection (ESD) method that independently groups and labels systematic errors made by a classification model. We demonstrate that this method is able to identify operationally-relevant artificially added perturbations in a non-military dataset. In a military context, our method clusters and describes images based on their surroundings, but also exhibits overlap between cluster descriptions. We further investigate the difference in embedding variation between our military and non-military dataset, which remains a topic of interest. Although the results do not yet warrant fully automated V&V through VLM-based ESD, they show that VLMs could be used to accelerate V&V processes in the future.

</details>


### [101] [ReconPlusGen: Injecting Reconstruction Prior into Multi-view 3D Generation through Noise Inversion and Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11129)
*Jiarui Liu,Heng Li,Weiyu Li,Keng Deng,Junyuan Deng,Zheng Zhongxing,Junyu Huang,Jiahao Chang,Xiaoguang Han,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文展示了多视图三维重建的定性结果，并提出一种"重建引导的噪声初始化与调制"机制：先将多张输入图像预测为规范空间中的点云，再通过噪声反演将预测几何确定性地注入扩散过程，并对所得噪声进行调制，以兼顾补全未见区域与精修可见几何所需的生成灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图/稀疏视图三维重建需要在"忠实还原观测几何"与"生成式补全未观测区域"之间取得平衡。纯生成式扩散模型虽能补全缺失区域，却难以严格遵循输入视图的几何约束；纯回归式重建方法虽几何确定，却缺乏生成灵活性。该工作试图同时满足这两点。

Method: 以多张输入图像为条件，先预测规范空间（canonical space）下的点云；随后通过噪声反演（noise inversion）把预测几何确定性地注入扩散采样过程，得到对应噪声；再对该噪声施加调制（noise modulation），在保留一定随机性的前提下引导采样，从而既锚定可见几何，又保留生成自由度。

Result: 在基准图像与真实场景图像上均给出了重建的定性结果（左上为基准图像重建，右上为真实图像重建），验证方法在受控与真实条件下的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过"预测点云—噪声反演注入—噪声调制"的流程，可将确定性几何先验与扩散生成能力结合，实现对观测区域的高保真重建与对未观测区域的合理补全。

Abstract: Qualitative results and an illustration of our core idea. Top left: reconstruction results on benchmark images. Top right: reconstruction results on real-world images. Bottom: illustration of reconstruction-guided noise initialization and modulation. Given multiple input images, we predict a point cloud in canonical space, deterministically inject the predicted geometry into the diffusion process through noise inversion, and modulate the resulting noise to preserve the generative flexibility required to complete unobserved regions and refine visible geometry.

</details>


### [102] [LAION-Mobile: Evaluating Deepfake Detectors On One Million Smartphone Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11134)
*Achim von Stryk,Janis Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAION-Mobile数据集（约100万张含EXIF的智能手机图像），并在9,115张（DIRE为738张）评测样本上评估12个SOTA深伪检测器，发现其在现代AI内容上AUC均不超过0.624、5个低于随机水平，且真实照片误报率高度依赖阈值校准，没有检测器能同时兼顾两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数深伪检测器在其参考基准上AUC接近完美，但已有ICML立场论文指出这些评估共同忽视了现代智能手机摄影的影响：设备端神经图像信号处理（多传感器融合、噪声与运动模糊抑制）使成像范式从简单镜头投影转向计算摄影，设备"生成"而非"记录"照片，从而增加了检测器将普通手机照片误判为伪造的风险。由于缺乏包含现代智能手机图像的大规模数据集，该假设此前仅在小规模概念验证研究中被检验，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从re-LAION-5B中蒸馏构建开放数据集LAION-Mobile，含约100万张带EXIF元数据的智能手机图像；使用12个SOTA深伪检测器的原始论文检查点，在9,115张图像的评测样本（DIRE仅用738张）上进行评估；通过对比在旧式GAN数据上拟合阈值与在现代内容上重新拟合相同判定标准，检验真实照片误报率与阈值校准的关系。

Result: (i) 在现代AI内容上，没有检测器AUC超过0.624，12个中有5个低于随机水平（chance）；(ii) 真实照片误报率是阈值校准的产物：在旧式GAN数据上拟合的阈值使多个检测器看似可用（FPR低于11%），但将同一判定标准在现代内容上重新拟合后，这些检测器会把17%–91%的真实照片判为伪造；(iii) 因此，没有检测器能同时做到在现代AI内容上优于随机且保持可部署的真实照片误报率。

Conclusion: 该语料镜像了网络收集数据的设备构成，主要考察第一代神经ISP时期（2018–2020）；当代旗舰机型基本缺失，现代ISP场景仍是尚未填补的空白，是未来研究的关键开放问题。

Abstract: Most Deepfake detectors report near-perfect AUC scores on their reference benchmarks. However, a recent ICML position paper argues that these evaluations collectively neglect the impact of modern smartphone photography: the widely used on-device neural image-signal processing pipelines (like multi-sensor fusion or noise and motion-blur suppression) increasingly shift the imaging paradigm from simple lens projections towards computational photography. Hence, devices actually generate, rather than record photos. This increases the risk that deepfake detectors may flag ordinary phone photos as fake. Due to the lack of large-scale datasets containing images from modern smartphones, this hypothesis has so far only been tested in small proof-of-concept studies. The aim of this paper is to close this gap. We introduce LAION-Mobile, an open dataset containing about 1 million smartphone images with EXIF metadata distilled from re-LAION-5B. Evaluating twelve state-of-the-art deepfake detectors with their original paper checkpoints on a 9,115-image evaluation sample of this pool (DIRE on 738), we report three key findings: (i) On modern AI content no detector exceeds AUC 0.624, and five of twelve fall below chance.
