Result: 零样本基础模型未稳定超越强基线；轻量微调显著提升性能，微调Chronos-Bolt在T1D队列RMSE降低6.5%–18.4%，在非糖尿病/T2D队列降低8.6%–18.2%，分布内与分布外测试均一致改善。CGMacros上残差融合相对仅CGM基线使整体RMSE降约3%、餐后RMSE降约15%；Chronos的CGM表征与餐后血糖增量相关性比LSTM、CatBoost更强，即使后者纳入膳食模态。

Conclusion: 基础模型需针对CGM进行适配才能实现可靠预测；膳食上下文在CGM之外提供具临床意义的信号，尤其在餐后时段。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides high-frequency measurements of glucose dynamics and enables short-term glucose forecasting for diabetes management. Although time-series foundation models have shown strong general forecasting ability, their effectiveness for CGM prediction and the added value of multimodal dietary context remain unclear. We conduct a comprehensive empirical study using eight public CGM datasets spanning Type 1 diabetes, Type 2 diabetes, and non-diabetes populations. Under a unified protocol across multiple context lengths and prediction horizons, zero-shot foundation models did not consistently outperform strong task-specific baselines such as Elastic Net and PatchTST. In contrast, lightweight fine-tuning substantially improved forecasting performance. For example, fine-tuned Chronos-Bolt reduced RMSE by 6.5%-18.4% in the T1D cohort and by 8.6%-18.2% in the non-diabetes/T2D cohort, with comparable improvements in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution test settings. We further evaluate multimodal dietary context using CGMacros, which provides temporally aligned CGM signals, food images, and macronutrient records. A residual-based fusion framework reduced overall RMSE by approximately 3% and postprandial RMSE by approximately 15% relative to the CGM-only baseline. Moreover, Chronos-based CGM representations were more strongly correlated with observed postprandial glucose increments than representations from LSTM and CatBoost, even after those models incorporated additional dietary modalities, suggesting that pretrained temporal representations better preserve meal-induced excursion patterns. These findings show that foundation models require CGM-specific adaptation for reliable forecasting and that dietary context provides clinically meaningful signals beyond CGM alone, especially during postprandial periods.

</details>


### [205] [Generative Marketing Mix Modeling: A Causal Inference Framework Linking GEO and GEM to Business Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11915)
*Masahiro Kato,Daiki Honma,Taka Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式营销组合建模（GMMM），用于估计生成引擎优化（GEO）与生成引擎营销（GEM）的因果效应，并通过英日双语模拟回答验证其经验表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI改变了企业触达客户的方式，但标准营销数据无法记录用户在生成式回答中看到并注意到企业名称的频率，导致GEO与GEM的因果效应难以度量。

Method: 提出GMMM框架：对GEO，整合重复生成的回答、问题计数、跨生成系统的使用份额及注意概率；对GEM，整合赞助投放记录与注意概率。通过比较不同处理序列下的期望业务响应，并给出识别相应效应的充分条件。

Result: 利用英语与日语的产品推荐模拟回答，对所提方法的经验表现进行了考察。

Conclusion: GMMM为生成式营销环境下GEO与GEM的因果效应估计提供了可行框架，并明确了效应可识别的充分条件。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence changes how firms reach customers, but standard marketing data do not record how often users see and notice a firm's name in generated answers. We develop Generative Marketing Mix Modeling (GMMM) to estimate the causal effects of Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) and Generative Engine Marketing (GEM). For GEO, GMMM combines repeated generated answers with question counts, shares of use across generative systems, and notice probabilities. For GEM, it combines records of sponsored placements with notice probabilities. GMMM compares expected business responses under alternative treatment sequences and establishes sufficient conditions for identifying the resulting effects. We investigate the empirical performance of the proposed method using simulated answers to product recommendation in English and Japanese.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [The Elliptically Optimal Confidence Interval: A Bivariate Extension of Wilson's Score Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10865)
*Nawaf Mohammed*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文针对两个独立二项比例之差 \(p_1-p_2\) 的区间估计，提出"椭圆最优（EO）置信区间"：将联合正态近似的椭圆区域投影到差值方向，得到闭式解（分六种互斥完备情形），并证明其等价于在冗余方差上取最大化所得的 score 区间，具有覆盖保证、精确覆盖超额公式与尺度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比例之差涉及一个估计量无法识别的冗余方向（nuisance direction）：单样本 Wilson score 区间可通过反演标量 score 检验构造，但对差值的二元情形没有直接类比——反演联合正态近似得到的是单位正方形内的椭圆区域，而估计量 \(p_1-p_2\) 是一维的。现有 Wald 区间在平衡分配下方差估计下偏（因子 \(1-1/n\)），几乎处处低于名义覆盖。因此需要一个既能隔离差值方向、又保留正态近似覆盖保证的区间构造。

Method: 定义 EO 区间为差值 \(p_1-p_2\) 在椭圆区域上的取值范围，并将该优化问题闭式求解，得到六种互斥且完备情形下的显式界。给出紧凑刻画：EO 区间等于在冗余方差上取最大化（而非估计）所得的 score 区间；推导精确覆盖超额公式 \(2[\Phi(z\mathcal{R})-\Phi(z)]\)，其中 \(\mathcal{R}\) 为最不利标准差与真实标准差之比。并对二项覆盖进行精确枚举，与 Wald 区间作对比。

Result: EO 区间是投影椭圆区域所得的最短区间，并继承其覆盖保证。覆盖超额在穿过参数空间的一条显式直线上为零，其上界为 \(\alpha\)，且在样本量成比例缩放时不变。精确枚举显示：平衡分配下 Wald 区间因方差估计下偏（\(1-1/n\)）而几乎处处低于名义覆盖；EO 区间在正态近似下从不过度不足覆盖，且始终给出可行、非退化的界。其代价是在两比例均极端时过度覆盖，作者对其作了精确量化。

Conclusion: EO 区间为独立二项比例之差提供了一种兼具闭式表达、覆盖保证与优良几何性质（最短投影）的区间估计方法；相较 Wald 区间在覆盖上的系统缺陷，EO 区间以在双端极端情形下的可量化过度覆盖为代价，换取从不过度不足覆盖的稳健性。

Abstract: Constructing a confidence interval for the difference between two independent binomial proportions involves a nuisance direction that is not identified by the estimand. The one-sample Wilson score interval inverts a scalar score test, but has no direct bivariate analogue isolating the difference: inverting the joint normal approximation yields an elliptical region in the unit square, whereas the estimand \(p_1-p_2\) is one-dimensional. We define the Elliptically Optimal (EO) confidence interval as the range of \(p_1-p_2\) over this region and solve the resulting optimization problem in closed form, obtaining explicit bounds in six mutually exclusive and exhaustive cases. The solution admits a compact characterization: the EO interval is the score interval obtained by maximizing over the nuisance variance rather than estimating it. It is therefore the shortest interval obtained by projecting the elliptical region, and inherits its coverage guarantee. We derive the exact coverage excess, \(2[Φ(z\mathcal{R})-Φ(z)]\), where \(\mathcal{R}\) is the ratio of the least-favourable to the true standard deviation. The excess vanishes on an explicit line through the parameter space, is bounded by \(α\), and is invariant under proportional scaling of the sample sizes. Exact enumeration of the binomial coverage shows that the Wald interval, whose variance estimator is downward biased by a factor \(1-1/n\) under balanced allocation, falls below nominal coverage almost everywhere. The EO interval never under-covers under the normal approximation and always yields admissible, non-degenerate bounds. Its price is over-coverage when both proportions are extreme, which we quantify exactly.

</details>


### [207] [Online Change-Point Monitoring for Object-valued Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10889)
*Yi Zhang,Tim Kutta,Xiaofeng Shao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出面向对象取值时间序列边际分布变化的闭端与开端监测方法，融合Hilbert空间距离嵌入、监测时间相关投影与自归一化，可从成对距离计算、无需估计长期方差、支持精确递推更新，并给出闭端/开端枢轴极限与临界值模拟表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象取值时间序列（如分布值、图值数据）的边际分布变化监测缺少通用且计算可行的方法；传统方法通常依赖向量化与长期方差估计，难以直接适用于此类复杂对象数据，且对无界监测时域缺乏理论保障。

Method: 方法结合两种基于距离的Hilbert空间嵌入、监测时间相关投影与自归一化；仅需成对距离即可计算，无需长期方差估计，并允许精确递归更新。闭端监测在弱时间相依下得到枢轴Brownian泛函极限；开端监测引入增长型监测时间权重，建立极大不等式与远端尾部一致控制，推导开端枢轴极限及用于临界值模拟的等价固定区间表示。

Result: 两类程序对固定边际变化具有一致性，并保持线性投影信号n^{-1/2}、二次投影信号n^{-1/4}的局部检测边界；分布值时间序列模拟显示良好数值性质，月度股票收益分布应用验证实用性；补充材料报告图值时间序列模拟与地震活动空间点过程应用。

Conclusion: 该框架为对象取值时间序列的边际分布变化监测提供了统一、计算友好且具理论保证的闭端与开端解决方案，覆盖分布值、图值与空间点过程等对象数据类型，并明确了局部检测边界与枢轴极限推断方式。

Abstract: We develop closed- and open-end procedures for monitoring changes in the marginal distribution of object-valued time series. The method combines two distance-based Hilbert-space embeddings, a monitoring-time-dependent projection, and self-normalization. It is computable entirely from pairwise distances, does not require long-run variance estimation, and admits exact recursive updates. Under weak temporal dependence, the closed-end null limit is a pivotal Brownian functional. For monitoring over an unbounded horizon, we introduce a growing monitoring-time weight, establish a maximal inequality and uniform remote-tail control, and derive an open-end pivotal limit together with an equivalent fixed-interval representation for critical-value simulation. Both procedures are consistent against fixed marginal changes and retain the $n^{-1/2}$ and $n^{-1/4}$ local detection boundaries associated with the linear and quadratic projection signals. Simulations with distribution-valued time series illustrate favorable numerical properties, and an application to monthly stock-return distributions illustrates the usefulness of our procedure. Further simulations with graph-valued time series and an application to spatial point processes of seismic activity are reported in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [208] [A Scale Invariance Property of PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11016)
*Jonathan Landy*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究PCA对测量尺度变化的敏感性问题，证明在主成分按特征值排序时，连续尺度调整下第k个主成分保持"序稳定性"，并解释了表观特征值交叉源于模态瞬时交换方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCA对测量尺度变化敏感：用英寸而非厘米度量变量会改变主轴和主特征值。这种尺度依赖性通常被认为复杂且缺乏规律，作者希望揭示其背后是否存在严格的结构性不变性质。

Method: 采用理论分析，考察连续尺度调整（scaling）下PCA模态的演化。将主成分按特征值大小排序，分析第k大主成分随尺度连续变化的行为；并专门分析沿与某些模态正交方向的缩放情形，讨论由此产生的表观特征值交叉（apparent eigenvalue crossings）。

Result: 证明了一个严格的不变性：在连续尺度调整下，初始状态的第k大主成分连续演变为最终状态的第k大主成分（对每个k成立），即PCA模态具有"序稳定性"（order-stable）。当沿正交于某些模态的方向缩放时，会出现表观特征值交叉；这些交叉可被解释为模态瞬时交换其方向，从而维持所需的序稳定性。

Conclusion: 尽管PCA对尺度变化总体敏感且依赖关系复杂，其主成分在连续尺度调整下遵循严格的序稳定性不变性；表观特征值交叉不影响该性质，因为它对应模态方向的瞬时交换。

Abstract: The PCA algorithm is sensitive to changes in measurement scale. Measuring one variable of a system in inches rather than centimeters, say, alters both its principal axes and principal eigenvalues. Although this scale dependence is generally complicated, we show here that it nevertheless obeys a strict invariance property: under a continuous scale adjustment, the initial state's $k$-th largest principal component (ordered by eigenvalue) continuously evolves into the final state's $k$-th largest principal component, for each $k$. In this sense, we can say that the modes of PCA are "order-stable" with respect to changes in measurement scale. A special case occurs when scaling along directions that are orthogonal to some modes. Here, apparent eigenvalue crossings can occur. However, we show that we can interpret these apparent crossings as cases where the modes instantaneously swap their orientation, in this way maintaining the required order stability.

</details>


### [209] [Small noise asymptotics for linear parabolic SPDEs in two space dimensions with unknown damping factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11162)
*Yozo Tonaki,Yusuke Kaino,Masayuki Uchida*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究二维空间中二阶线性抛物型随机偏微分方程的参数估计问题，基于高频时空数据，提出了阻尼参数、扩散参数、平流参数和反应参数的估计量，并给出模拟结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带有未知阻尼参数Q-Wiener过程驱动的、含小波动率参数的二维二阶线性抛物型SPDE，现有方法缺乏系统的参数估计框架，尤其是结合高频时空数据的估计问题尚未充分解决。

Method: 首先利用基于空间和时间增量的实现二次变差构造Q-Wiener过程阻尼参数的估计量；其次使用含该阻尼参数估计量的对比函数，构造扩散参数和平流参数的极小对比估计量；最后基于扩散和平流参数估计量导出的近似坐标过程，构造反应参数的拟极大似然估计量。

Result: 提供了所提出各估计量的模拟结果，验证了方法的可行性与估计性能。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套完整的SPDE参数估计流程，涵盖阻尼、扩散、平流和反应参数的估计，为高频时空数据下的二阶线性抛物型SPDE参数推断提供了有效方法。

Abstract: We study parametric estimation for second order linear parabolic stochastic partial differential equations in two space dimensions with a small volatility parameter driven by a $Q$-Wiener process with an unknown damping parameter using high frequency spatio-temporal data. We first provide an estimator for the damping parameter of the $Q$-Wiener process utilizing realized quadratic variations based on spatial and temporal increments. We next propose minimum contrast estimators of the diffusive and advective parameters in the SPDE using a contrast function with the proposed estimator of the damping parameter. We then construct a quasi-maximum likelihood estimator of the reaction parameter in the SPDE using the approximate coordinate process derived from the estimators of the diffusive and advective parameters. We also provide simulation results of the proposed estimators.

</details>


### [210] [Spike Estimation from Heteroscedastic Noise via Random Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11169)
*Zhigang Bao,Kha Man Cheong,Yuji Li,Jiaxin Qiu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 针对异构未知方差剖面的尖峰Wigner矩阵，提出"非对称化"方法（随机二分），将Hermitian噪声矩阵转化为非Hermitian矩阵但保留尖峰，建立BBP型相变从而实现尖峰强度的精确估计，并推广到两个相关尖峰模型间信号相关性的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同方差异构（heteroscedastic）且方差剖面未知的情形下，超临界BBP相变中强尖峰虽会产生谱离群值，但离群值是含未知方差剖面参数的Dyson方程的解，导致无法由观测离群值一致地估计尖峰强度。因此需要一种不依赖方差剖面知识的估计方法。

Method: 受稀疏矩阵补全工作（Bordenave, Coste, Nadakuditi 2023）启发，引入"非对称化"模型：将尖峰矩阵随机分裂为两部分，把带噪Wigner型矩阵转化为非Hermitian随机矩阵，在大幅稀释的同时保留Hermitian尖峰结构。对该非对称化模型建立BBP型相变，据此精确估计尖峰强度，且无需已知噪声部分的方差剖面。进一步应用于两个相关尖峰模型（信号部分相关、噪声独立但可能均异构），分别及联合施加非对称化，估计两模型信号部分之间的相关性。

Result: 建立了非对称化模型的BBP型相变；在该相变下可精确估计尖峰强度，无需知道噪声的方差剖面；并成功将该方法用于估计两个相关尖峰模型信号部分之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 通过随机二分引入的非对称化方法，绕开了异构未知方差剖面下Dyson方程不可解的问题，实现了尖峰强度的无参数一致估计，并可拓展至双相关尖峰模型的信号相关性估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a spiked Wigner type matrix with a heteroscedastic and unknown variance profile. It is well known that in the supercritical regime of the BBP transition, strong spikes can create outliers in the spectrum. Unfortunately, in the heteroscedastic case, in general it is not possible to estimate the spike strength from these observed outlier consistently, as the latter is a solution to a Dyson equation with unknown parameters from the variance profile. In this paper, inspired by the work on sparse matrix completion \citep{BordenaveCosteNadakuditi2023}, we introduce an asymmetrized model by randomly splitting the spiked matrix into two parts, which transforms the noisy Wigner type matrix into a non Hermitian random matrix, while preserving the Hermitian spikes at the cost of a dilution. We establish a BBP type transition for the asymmetrized model, from which we can estimate the strength of the spikes precisely, even without knowing the variance profile of the noise part. We then further apply our approach to study the correlation between two correlated spiked models, where the spike/signal parts of the two models are correlated, and the noise parts are independent but may both be heteroscedastic. By applying our asymmetrization approach to the two models separately and also jointly, we are able to obtain a precise estimate of the correlation between the signal parts of the two models.

</details>


### [211] [Dynamical Clustering and its Limiting Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11608)
*Paul A. Milewski,Esteban G. Tabak*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一种基于软分配空间流的新型非参数聚类算法，结合非线性反应与自适应扩散，扩展到半监督分类及多扩散网络应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对聚类中类别边界模糊、扩散尺度难以自适应的问题，以及半监督分类和高维聚类（维度灾难）的挑战，寻求一种能自发形成清晰边界、平衡各类域的通用框架。

Method: 在软分配 $P_k^i$（点 $x^i$ 属类 $k$ 的概率）空间中构造流：一为非线性反应项，将 $P$ 松弛到演化的类贝叶斯后验；二为扩散项，将 $P$ 松弛到局部平均；扩散系数 $ν$ 动态自适应以平衡两项。方法可扩展至半监督分类（部分标签已知）；引入多个扩散网络可支持时间序列变点检测及多特征聚类。

Result: 在无穷观测极限下导出一组非标准反应-扩散方程，可产生物种间清晰边界并自发分离为均衡的域；多扩散网络扩展得到一类新型非局部扩散算子，并在时间序列状态变化检测与高维特征聚类中克服维度灾难。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了聚类、半监督分类、变点检测与高维聚类，为反应-扩散型聚类及非局部扩散算子提供了理论与应用基础。

Abstract: A novel, non-parametric clustering algorithm is developed, based on flows in the space of soft assignments $P_k^i$, representing the probability that point $x^i$ belongs to class $k$. These flows have two drivers: a nonlinear reaction component that relaxes $P$ to an evolving Bayesian-like posterior, and a diffusive component that relaxes $P$ to its local average, with a diffusivity $ν$ that adapts dynamically so as to balance the two components. The methodology extends to semi-supervised classification, where a subset of the labels are known. In the limit of infinitely many observations, it yields a set of non-standard reaction-diffusion equations, which produces sharp boundaries between species that separate spontaneously into well-balanced domains. Including more than one diffusive network opens the way to a broader class of applications, including the detection of regime changes in time series and a clustering procedure based on numerous features that defeats the curse of dimensionality. In the continuous limit, this extension results in a novel, non-local class of diffusive operators.

</details>


### [212] [Gromov-Wasserstein Barycenter Surrogates: Statistical Methodology, Distributional Limits and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11670)
*Florian Steinkamp,Luis-Alberto Rodríguez,Jan Victor Otte,Axel Munk*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文基于Gromov-Wasserstein距离的第二下界（SLB），提出了针对有限多个度量测度空间（mm-space）匹配的统计理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量测度空间匹配缺乏可计算的统计推断框架，尤其需要识别各mm-space内部（成对）距离分布偏差的方法。

Method: 基于Gromov-Wasserstein距离的第二下界（SLB）；引入易于计算且可用各物体距离分布显式表达的SLB重心代理；对m个mm-space各取n个随机样本时，统计量可在O(m·n²log(n))基本操作内高效计算。

Result: 推导了所提检验统计量的渐近分布与有限样本界，为统计推断提供了基础，包括姿态不变物体判别的渐近检验以及基于SLB重心的、具可控错误率的分类方法。

Conclusion: 方法通过模拟研究验证，并应用于蛋白质结构域的结构比较。

Abstract: We introduce statistical theory for the matching of finitely many objects, represented as metric measure spaces (mm-spaces). The approach is based on the second lower bound (SLB) of the Gromov-Wasserstein distance and thus is able to identify deviations in the distributions of the (pairwise) distances within each mm-space. We introduce a surrogate of the SLB barycenter which can be easily computed and expressed explicitly in terms of the distance distributions of each object. When comparing $m$ mm-spaces for $n$ randomly drawn samples in each space, the resulting statistic then can be calculated efficiently in $O(m \cdot n^2 \log(n))$ basic operations. We derive the asymptotic distribution and finite-sample bounds of the proposed test statistic, which serves as a basis for a variety of tools for statistical inference, specifically an asymptotic test for pose-invariant object discrimination and a classification method (based on the SLB barycenter) with controlled error rates. These methods are investigated in simulations and applied to the structural comparison of protein domains.

</details>


### [213] [From Good Starts to Optimal Inference: Generalized Latent Factor Models with Missingness and Implicit Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11740)
*Chengzhu Huang,Yuqi Gu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为带缺失值的非线性潜因子模型（指数族链接）建立了从非凸估计算法到统计推断的理论桥梁，提出链接感知双SVD初始化、单边精化与梯度下降相结合的过程，并给出潜因子与缺失项均值的渐近有效置信区间/区间带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义潜因子模型分析高维非高斯数据灵活，但在缺失情形下的有原则估计与不确定性量化发展不足；现有工作缺乏对非线性潜因子模型缺失数据的可计算估计与统计推断之间的理论连接。

Method: 结合链接感知双SVD初始化、达到逐行一致性的单边精化与普通梯度下降；证明精化初始化器进入非相干且收缩的区域，梯度下降通过隐式正则保持在区域内并快速收缩至统计估计误差，无需显式非相干或平衡正则；核心结果是梯度下降实际输出的逐行均匀线性近似。

Result: 该线性近似将主导得分波动与高阶估计/优化误差分离，从而无需额外去偏即可得到潜因子的渐近有效个体推断与高斯乘子自助同时推断，以及缺失项均值的同时置信带；估计率在忽略对数因子时匹配受限类极小极大下界，理论可容纳严重缺失、弱低秩信号与递减局部曲率。

Conclusion: 仿真支持理论发现，并应用于大语言模型评测，实现对潜在模型能力的不确定性感知估计与排序。

Abstract: Generalized latent factor models provide a flexible framework for analyzing high-dimensional non-Gaussian data, but principled estimation and uncertainty quantification under missingness remain substantially less developed. We develop a theory that connects a computationally tractable nonconvex procedure directly to statistical inference for nonlinear latent factor models with exponential-family links and partially observed entries. Our procedure combines a link-aware double-SVD initialization, a unilateral refinement that achieves rowwise consistency, and vanilla gradient descent. We show that the refined initializer enters a region of incoherence and contraction and that gradient descent remains in this region through implicit regularization, contracting rapidly down to the statistical estimation error without explicit incoherence or balancing regularization. Our central result is a uniform rowwise linear approximation for the actual output of gradient descent that isolates the leading score fluctuations from higher-order estimation and optimization errors. These expansions yield asymptotically valid individual and Gaussian multiplier-bootstrap simultaneous inference for latent factors, together with simultaneous confidence bands for missing-entry means, without requiring an additional debiasing step. The resulting estimation rate matches a restricted-class minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors, while the theory accommodates severe missingness, weak low-rank signals, and diminishing local curvature. Simulations support the theoretical findings, and an application to large language model evaluation illustrates uncertainty-aware estimation and ranking of latent model capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M3-Former，利用LLM增强的多模态框架进行船舶轨迹预测，借助语义先验与双粒度MoE，在丹麦AIS数据集1-4小时预测上超越SOTA，4小时任务ADE/FDE分别降低4.4%和5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶轨迹预测面临三大挑战：行为多模态性、语义利用率有限以及长期误差累积。现有方法未充分利用船舶静态属性与航行意图等语义信息，难以支撑长期轨迹建模。

Method: 1) 构建统一多模态表示空间，将船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验，用预训练LLM编码静态语义，并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；2) 设计双粒度混合专家（MoE）架构，序列级专家建模全局航行趋势，token级专家细化局部操纵行为，兼顾全局路径规划与局部运动变化；3) 提出转向加权交叉熵损失（Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy），缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布问题。

Result: 在真实丹麦AIS数据集上，M3-Former在1至4小时各预测时域上均稳定优于SOTA基线；4小时预测任务中ADE和FDE分别较最强基线降低4.4%和5.1%。定性与消融分析表明，语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度MoE提升复杂水道与航线分支场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的分层预测范式，将高层航行意图与局部运动动态联合建模，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [215] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Halo方法，通过在深度预测器中增加尺度参数输出并采用负对数似然训练，实现异方差预测，不仅用于不确定性量化，还能提升点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常基于不确定性量化的动机，而时序以外的异方差估计存在负面结果，本文旨在验证异方差预测能否改善点估计。

Method: Halo复用现有深度预测器架构，增加一个预测隐含分布尺度的输出，并用匹配的负对数似然训练；在transformer、图网络+变分自编码器、单层卷积网络上，分别采用高斯和拉普拉斯损失进行适配。

Result: 在标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场上，30个模型-市场-指标对比中有28个的MSE和MAE得到改善，平均MSE降低2.6%至16.5%，平均MAE降低1.7%至11.0%。

Conclusion: 网络是否估计尺度比尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络更重要；且在已为点估计基线调优的超参数下改进依然成立，无需重新调参。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [216] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将预训练文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样融入密度法拓扑优化的物理循环，使文本提示成为可机器解释的设计意图表示，在多数提示-域组合中显著降低柔度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载构型（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化器很少达到此类几何，且鲜有方法支持工程师用自然语言表达结构设计意图。

Method: 以冻结的文本到图像扩散模型为免训练先验，借助分数蒸馏采样将其蒸馏进密度法拓扑优化的物理循环；每次迭代将提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度结合，由物理决定保留哪些生成特征。跨域迁移仅需更换文本提示并单独设定各问题物理设置；采用带β延拓的Heaviside投影进行离散化，并构建自动化骨架提取流程将优化密度场转为可供CAD使用的候选几何。

Result: 在覆盖四个几何域、两种物理机制的245次主SDS运行中，49个提示-域组合有38个实现统计显著的柔度下降（力学最高-31.5%，热弹性-23.0%），优于梯度类基线。跨域形态分析发现反复出现的改进结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制肋骨架的盲端分支，端点-柔度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99。带β延拓的Heaviside投影将中间密度倾向从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可跨域、跨载荷与跨物理目标重定向生成先验，且各新问题物理设置可独立指定，该框架证明预训练生成模型可作为可复用、免训练的工程设计先验。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [217] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端与固定聚合服务器单点故障三大障碍，提出数据集、压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒FL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测依赖边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中收集，故需联邦学习；但现有联邦方案未解决上行带宽受限、拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端，以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任这三个实际问题。

Method: (i) 从八个公开来源构建经过整理的室内火灾检测数据集；(ii) 设计可边缘部署的检测器，将模型更新压缩至最多10倍且仅带来少量平衡准确率损失；(iii) 提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者，以剔除逐轮过滤漏掉的隐蔽攻击。

Result: 在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法在准确率和检测速度上与固定服务器方案相当；物理分布的六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方案同时缓解带宽、拜占庭攻击与单点故障问题，且在保持性能的同时实现可部署性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [218] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)，在源空间有标注校准、目标空间仅有配对无标注数据的设定下，将源校准迁移至目标空间并做残差校正，实现目标域覆盖率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测依赖校准数据与部署数据的可交换性，但实际中标注校准仅存在于源空间，而预测集需在通过无标注配对观测（如配对模态、传感器变化）关联的目标空间使用，可交换性失效。

Method: 先将有标注的源校准集通过配对数据迁移到目标空间，再仅利用无标注目标输入校正迁移后的残差失配。两种实例化：TCC-KS用无标签不确定性代理检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC在权重稳定时对迁移校准按目标域重加权以提升效率。

Result: 给出适应可观测失配度量的有限样本目标域覆盖保证；在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C、SEN12MS上，无需标注目标校准数据即实现可靠的目标域覆盖率迁移，且无标签诊断能预测何时需要校正。

Conclusion: TCC在源-目标空间失配场景下实现了无需目标标注的共形校准迁移，兼具理论覆盖保证与实证有效性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [219] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015年以来人工智能与深度学习在放射学肺癌检测中的应用，指出CNN配合迁移学习与数据增强是提升诊断准确性与效率的有前景技术，同时强调需解决数据标准化、模型可解释性、隐私与伦理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善预后和生活质量至关重要；但医学影像判读过程复杂且依赖专业受训人员，因此需要借助AI与深度学习实现影像分析的自动化与优化。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出2015年至今的96篇探讨AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的文章进行综述，重点分析卷积神经网络（CNN）结合迁移学习和数据增强等技术。

Result: 结果表明，AI与深度学习在肺癌早期诊断中可提供有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感性和特异性，其中CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强被视为提升影像判读准确性与效率的有前景技术。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习可对肺癌患者诊疗产生积极影响，但当前仍存在标准化数据缺乏、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私以及伦理和社会影响等局限与挑战，需进一步研究与监管以确保其临床应用的质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [220] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续流匹配与离散流匹配之间的一种对偶关系，并据此把连续源分布作为类别生成的一个显式设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流匹配与离散流匹配通常被当作两套彼此独立的构造来处理，二者之间缺乏统一的理论联系，同时也缺少对离散生成中源分布设计的系统理据。

Method: 通过对带one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径施加逐位置argmax投影，将其映射为离散凸插值路径。该对偶成立需源分布满足相应的坐标对称性与边界正则性。作者进一步推导Gaussian、有界均匀以及中心化负指数三类源所诱导的离散插值行为。

Result: 不同源几何会导致在转移时序与词表规模依赖上出现定性差异；小规模可视化诊断与一个简短的语言建模试验表明，这些源设计效应在学习到的传输以及早期生成质量中亦可观察到。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配可由argmax投影联系起来，连续源分布因而成为类别生成中一个可显式选择的设计维度，并会影响转移时序、词表规模依赖及实际生成质量。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [221] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GeoSteer，将保范激活引导建模为黎曼流形上的优化问题，通过自适应多步测地线更新实现更稳定、更有效的LLM行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法受限于预定义的引导轨迹与单步更新，难以刻画激活分布的复杂结构，可能引发表征塌缩与性能退化。

Method: GeoSteer 将引导视为黎曼优化问题，在表征流形上沿一系列小测地线步长更新激活；学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数以区分期望与非期望激活，并据此自适应地指导每一步引导，避免固定引导方向。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts 与 UltraFeedback 三个基准上，GeoSteer 持续优于当前最先进的激活引导基线，同时保持激活范数不变。

Conclusion: 用自适应、几何感知的优化替代预定义的单步编辑，可使保范激活引导更有效；多步黎曼更新带来更平滑、稳定且一致的引导行为。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [222] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究标准初始化下多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动态，证明增量学习仍然发生，并揭示参数质量的竞争性再分配现象；技术上引入基于对称性的有限宽度近似框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于浅层网络增量学习的研究多依赖小初始化、相关损失或逐层训练等简化设定，这些设定削弱了神经元间的相互作用，导致标准初始化下的特征学习动态尚未被充分探索。作者希望在更贴近实际训练的标准初始化、多项式宽度、多项式样本条件下，刻画两层网络学习正交多指标目标的完整动态。

Method: 针对正交多指标目标，在标准初始化与多项式样本量下分析两层网络的训练动态。理论分析采用略作修改的梯度流，并引入基于对称性的对称化网络来构造有限宽度近似，替代直接与无限宽度极限比较，从而更精细地控制近似误差。实验中用普通梯度下降验证定性动态一致。

Result: 证明增量学习仍然发生：损失按目标的Hermite展开依次下降，低阶分量先于高阶分量被学习，进而恢复各个目标方向。标准初始化下还出现参数质量的竞争性再分配：总质量先拟合目标均值并稳定，随后质量转入目标子空间，最终集中到与目标对齐的神经元上。普通梯度下降在经验上呈现相同的定性动态。

Conclusion: 标准初始化下的多项式宽度两层网络同样表现出增量学习与参数质量的竞争性再分配，说明这些现象不依赖于小初始化等简化设定。所提出的基于对称性的有限宽度近似方法具有独立的分析价值。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [223] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"相对智能学习"：ERM 及任意恰当一致学习器在二分类中相对智能；半监督下可实现仅无标注样本二次膨胀、有标注样本零膨胀；但该标注效率以计算复杂度为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dughmi 与 Pour 提出的相对智能学习框架中，已知 One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) 学习器具有二次样本复杂度膨胀且为最优，但 ERM 或其他自然/可计算学习器能否达到类似保证、以及膨胀能否仅局限于无标注数据，均未解决。

Method: 证明 ERM 及任意恰当一致学习器在分布无关二分类设定下相对智能：由小可认证误差推导出在 O(m^2) 随机样本均匀分布上的小误差，得到大小至多 2^{m+1} 的覆盖；将 OIG 推广到 leave-most-out 转导问题以实现半监督学习；并通过 agnostic ERM oracle 调用次数下界分析刻画其复杂性。

Result: ERM 及任意恰当一致学习器以 O(m^2) 样本实现相对智能；半监督相对智能学习可仅在无标注样本复杂度上二次膨胀、有标注样本无膨胀；若仅通过 agnostic ERM oracle 访问假设类，任何有标注样本膨胀显著次二次的半监督相对智能学习器需超多项式次 oracle 调用。

Conclusion: 相对智能学习可由更自然的恰当一致学习器实现，突破了对 OIG 的依赖；半监督设定下可将膨胀转移到无标注数据，实现标注效率；但该标注效率以牺牲简洁性与可计算性为代价，并给出分布固定学习的一个独立不可处理性结果。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [224] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证"完美混叠"现象——当真实报告与任务规定动作在同一情境中重合时，仅凭拟合标签无法区分二者；通过随机码本与混合情境拟合可以解开混叠，并在Gemma-2-9B策略上实现从0.006到1.000 AUROC的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真值探针（truth probe）在"真实报告"与"任务规定动作"重合的合规情境中拟合时，其标签本身无法区分所测目标究竟是"真实性"还是"被规定的动作"。这一语义识别失败在需要检测欺骗行为的场景中具有根本性风险：探针可能只是学到了任务规定的输出符号，而非信念状态。论文旨在刻画这一混叠机制、给出可检验的数学身份，并探索解耦路径。

Method: 1) 在受控二元报告博弈中，用合规情境拟合真值探针与规定动作探针，证明二者求解同一优化问题；2) 在对手情境中二者标签互为补集，推导出 AUROC 之和恒为1 的身份，并在751个 cell-layer 组合上以浮点精度验证；3) 用随机码本将"规定输出符号"与"语义动作"分离；4) 通过在合规与对手情境的混合样本上拟合，将"真值"与"规定动作"分离；5) 在奖励训练的 Gemma-2-9B 策略上比较传统探针与混合拟合探针的表现。

Result: 在对手试验中全部给出虚假回答的 Gemma-2-9B 策略上，传统探针在三个训练种子下 AUROC 仅为 0.006±0.005，而混合拟合探针在同一批留出激活上达到 1.000。两个分布内均完美的合规拟合探针，在同一对手激活上分别得分 0.080 与 0.986。AUROC 互补身份在751个 cell-layer 对上精确成立。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了探针"测量的是什么"这一语义识别问题：完美混叠会使合规拟合探针无法区分真值与规定动作。混合拟合虽实现了线性可分性，但使用了更多训练样本并可访问带标签的对手情境，因此该对比仅证明线性可恢复性，而非隔离去相关带来的收益。全文未证明功能性信念的保留、所恢复方向的因果作用，也未提供可部署的欺骗检测器。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [225] [AUC Maximization from Biased Positive-unlabeled Data with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10928)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在有偏正例-无标签(PU)数据下最大化AUC的方法，通过利用与少量标注正例相关联的置信度来修正偏差，并证明其可诱导贝叶斯最优排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUC最大化是类别不平衡二分类的标准方法，通常需要正负样本。但在隐私或专业标注受限的实际场景中，负样本难以收集，因此PU数据下的AUC最大化受到关注。现有方法假设标注正例是真实正类分布的无偏采样，而该理想假设在实践中常被违背。

Method: 针对有偏PU数据，核心思想是利用置信度（即实例为正的概率），将其与少量标注正例关联。基于带置信度的有偏PU数据，推导出AUC风险估计量，从而实现在偏差情形下的AUC最大化。

Result: 理论分析表明，重写后的AUC风险即使在可用置信度是真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换时，也能诱导出贝叶斯最优的AUC排序。在八个真实数据集上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法放宽了标注正例无偏采样的强假设，将有偏PU数据下的AUC最大化转化为可求解的风险估计问题，并具备对置信度单调变换的鲁棒性，实验证明了其实用性。

Abstract: Maximizing the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is a standard approach to imbalanced binary classification. Although positive and negative data are required for maximizing the AUC, negative data are often difficult to collect in some real-world applications due to privacy concerns or the need for specialized expertise to annotate them. Thus, AUC maximization from positive and unlabeled (PU) data has been attracting attention. Existing methods assume that labeled positive data are unbiased samples from the true positive distribution. However, this ideal assumption is often violated in practice. In this paper, we propose a method to maximize the AUC from biased PU data. To address the bias, our key idea is to exploit {\it confidence}, i.e., the probability that an instance is positive, associated with the small number of labeled positive data. We derive an estimator of the AUC risk using biased PU data with confidence, enabling AUC maximization under such bias. We further show that the rewritten AUC risk induces a Bayes-optimal AUC ranking even when the available confidence is any strictly increasing transformation of the true posterior probability. We experimentally show the effectiveness of our method on eight real-world datasets.

</details>


### [226] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应边界序数损失（AMOL）以抑制"中心类对冲"现象，并引入中心对冲率（CHR）诊断指标，在四个序数分类基准上取得最优或并列最优的QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使预测向中心类偏移，因为预测中间类可最小化期望对称损失，从而成为"阻力最小路径"，即"中心类对冲"。现有序数损失虽能处理大误差惩罚、秩一致性等问题，但均未针对"真实标签相对序数中心的位置"来直接抑制该现象。

Method: 提出AMOL：对逐类损失项施加乘性权重 m(k,y) = 1 + α·(1 - |k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中c为中心类、k为候选类、y为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件——仅当候选类靠近中心且真实标签远离中心时权重较大，其余情况退化为标准行为。同时引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标，并设计非对称变体AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子下，AMOL在全部四个数据集上的QWK均达到最优或并列最优，优于交叉熵、OLL和SORD基线。AMOL-asym在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（五个种子CHR = 0.000 ± 0.000，每次运行约266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074 ± 0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过刻画真实标签相对序数中心的位置，有效抑制中心类对冲，配合CHR指标可定量验证其效果，为序数分类提供更稳健的损失设计。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [227] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Krotov-Hopfield二部架构（类H模型），用统计物理方法统一分析多项式与指数存储容量的检索行为，并揭示可见层与隐藏层在检索中的分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield网络的联想记忆是多体无序系统中的吸引子动力学，其高阶与指数扩展将检索更新转化为softmax注意力。然而多项式与指数两种regime此前用不同方法分析，缺乏统一架构来追问存储尺度由什么决定，因此作者希望在共同框架下研究这一问题。

Method: 采用Krotov-Hopfield二部架构（记为类H），每层由拉格朗日量固定，将隐藏神经元视为检索的序参量。多项式负载下用replica方法求解replica-symmetric相图并给出闭式容量；指数负载下用softmax隐藏层与copy representation，将热力学映射为随机能量模型计数问题。

Result: 多项式负载下得到replica-symmetric相图与闭式容量，Ising与球面可见神经元的crosstalk矩相同，差异来自可见熵；softmax隐藏层给出指数负载，并呈现顺磁、凝聚、冻结三相。加热通过注意力的量子化重分配破坏检索，典型高斯模式在各负载下均保持亚稳。两种regime的crosstalk统计不同：多项式负载为中央极限，指数负载为大偏差。

Conclusion: 类H将检索分解为两个角色：可见拉格朗日量决定稳定性，隐藏拉格朗日量决定存储尺度，这两条轴也可指导新拉格朗日量的设计。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [228] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将持续强化学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡重新表述为"赋权-可塑性权衡"，并首次尝试在新定义下优化可塑性，证明了存在类似赋权的Bellman最优性方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中稳定性-可塑性权衡是核心难题。Abel等(2025)将可塑性定义为观测到动作的广义有向信息，将赋权定义为动作到观测的广义有向信息，从而把传统权衡重构为赋权-可塑性权衡。然而现有文献多聚焦赋权优化，尚无研究在此新定义下优化可塑性。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程框架下开展对可塑性优化的初步研究，并推导针对可塑性的Bellman最优性方程。

Result: 证明了在MDP中存在一个用于优化可塑性的Bellman最优性方程，其形式与既有赋权优化工作类似。

Conclusion: 该工作为在新定义下优化可塑性提供了初步理论基础，弥补了赋权-可塑性权衡中可塑性优化研究的空白。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [229] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无标签-无标签（UU）学习的分布偏移自适应方法，基于重要性加权，仅用训练分布中的UU数据及少量测试分布UU数据即可最小化测试风险，统一处理PU、噪声标签等分布偏移下的学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UU学习假设测试与训练分布具有相同的类条件密度，但现实中因分布偏移该假设常不成立；且现有重要性加权方法无法处理UU数据，已有偏移适应方法通常仅针对特定问题定制。

Method: 提出基于重要性加权的UU学习分布偏移自适应方法：利用训练分布中的UU数据和测试分布中的少量UU数据，估计重要性权重，并用带权训练数据最小化测试风险；理论上证明可在UU数据下以有原则的方式完成权重估计，且不依赖协变量偏移等偏移类型假设。

Result: 在真实数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性；借助UU学习的通用性，可在单一框架内处理PU学习、噪声标签学习等在分布偏移下的多种问题。

Conclusion: 该工作将重要性加权扩展到UU学习场景，实现了无需偏移类型假设、通用且统一的分布偏移自适应学习框架。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [230] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实边缘化(CF marginalisation)作为测试时评估方法，用以衡量分类模型对人口统计学/采集相关"捷径"变量的鲁棒性，并定义CF风险、校准、稳定性与最坏情况敏感度四类指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能借助人口统计学或采集相关的捷径(shortcut)变量获得良好测试性能，这种非因果依赖在分布偏移下会失效。现有评估缺乏针对此类 nuisance 变量鲁棒性的量化手段。

Method: 给定一个反事实图像生成器，对语义父变量(parent variables)如年龄、性别进行干预，为每张测试图像生成反事实版本，并在目标干预分布上对预测取平均，得到干预感知预测。该预测边缘化人口统计学效应，同时保留患者个体化潜变量信息。基于此预测定义CF风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感度等指标。

Result: 框架能够对不同模型的鲁棒性进行定量评估：通过干预后预测分布刻画模型对 nuisance 变量的依赖程度，四个指标分维度揭示风险、置信度可靠性、预测稳定性及最坏情形下的敏感度。

Conclusion: CF边缘化是一种可行且实用的测试时鲁棒性评估流程，可用于定量比较分类模型对人口统计学与采集相关捷径变量的稳健性。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [231] [How Wrong Can a Good Predictor Be? Diverging Updates with Vanishing Predictive KL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11132)
*Qifu Wen,Shuaijun Liu,Zihan Zhou,Xi Zeng,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在平稳对称高斯HMM中，即使精确贝叶斯更新与确定性径向滤波器的更新映射之间存在无界差距，其对固定有限$K\ge2$的预测KL仍可趋于零，说明内部更新差距本身并不必然导致预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有观点常以内部贝叶斯更新映射的近似误差来推断预测失效，但"内部更新差距"与"预测代价"之间缺乏严格联系。本文旨在证明二者可以解耦：无界的更新映射差距可以与消失的预测KL共存。

Method: 在平稳对称高斯HMM中，令精确贝叶斯混合与一个显式确定性径向滤波器作用于相同的$K-1$个信念坐标；构造显式见证，分析$q\to0^+$时中心logit中最坏情形差距随自然置信尺度$L_K(q)$至少线性增长，而类别$D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$在同一位证上趋于零。分析平稳轨迹上滤波后验的期望终端KL，并用等距高斯扫描$K\in\{2,4,8\}$及二值对照验证。

Result: 最坏情形下中心logit差距随$L_K(q)$至少线性增长（无界），而$D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$趋于零；平稳轨迹上期望终端KL以$H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$收敛到零。无开关的典型块将两滤波器驱入共同置信锥，softmax曲率抑制分歧，单一高斯极大事件控制自适应噪声；扫描实验展示了相反趋势，二值对照比较了饱和与非饱和递推。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了内部更新差距与预测代价之间缺失的两个环节：分离状态对期望损失的贡献以及解码器敏感性，表明无界内部更新差距并不能单独证明预测失败。该构造固定$K$，不能给出压缩何时无害的通用判据，也未刻画内部差距何时必然带来任务损失。

Abstract: Accurate posterior prediction need not require accurate approximation of Bayesian updates. We prove that an unbounded gap between the update maps can coexist with vanishing predictive KL for every fixed finite $K\ge2$ in a stationary symmetric Gaussian HMM. Exact Bayesian mixing and an explicit deterministic radial filter act on the same $K-1$ belief coordinates. As $q\to0^+$, their separation in centered logits in the worst case grows at least linearly in the natural confidence scale $L_K(q)$, while their categorical $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$ vanishes at the same explicit witness. Along stationary HMM trajectories, the expected terminal KL between filtered posteriors also converges to zero at $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$. Typical blocks without switches drive both filters into a common confidence cone, where softmax curvature suppresses their disagreement; a single Gaussian maximal event controls adaptive noise. A sweep with equally spaced Gaussians over $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ illustrates the opposing trends, and binary controls at long horizons compare saturating and nonsaturating recurrences. The result isolates two missing links between internal update gaps and predictive cost: the contribution of separating states to expected loss and decoder sensitivity. Thus even an unbounded internal update gap does not by itself certify predictive failure. The construction is fixed in $K$ and does not provide a universal criterion for when compression is harmless or characterize when internal gaps must incur task loss.

</details>


### [232] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出G2QDR框架，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度，并将其转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，为GCHRL提供多层次连续引导，可理论集成到任意现有GCHRL架构并在稀疏奖励环境中提升基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图与目标条件分层强化学习（GCHRL）结合的方法多将图视为随机采样工具，忽视了图所蕴含的内在连通性信息，未能充分利用底层拓扑结构进行高效学习。该问题在拟度量（quasimetric）环境中尤为突出，因状态转移的固有非对称性对稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划构成根本性挑战。

Method: 引入状态连通性模型，用于预测非对称环境中成对状态的连通强度；将该连通强度转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，在多个分层层级上提供连续引导。状态连通性模型基于探索过程中生成的有向状态图，通过神经网络高效实现。作者证明该框架可理论集成到任意现有GCHRL架构中。

Result: 在广泛的稀疏奖励环境上的实验表明，G2QDR总体能够以可接受的计算开销增强基线GCHRL方法的性能。

Conclusion: G2QDR通过挖掘图的连通性信息并将其转化为稠密奖励，有效缓解了拟度量环境中非对称性带来的学习困难，且具备良好的通用性与可集成性，为图引导的分层强化学习提供了一种新思路。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [233] [Hierarchical Clustering Can Jointly Satisfy Richness, Consistency, and Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11173)
*Daichi Kuroda,Maximilien Dreveton,Matthias Grossglauser,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究聚类不可能性定理在层次聚类下的表现，证明三条公理（尺度不变性、丰富性、一致性）在层次框架下可同时满足，并刻画了可容许方法的偏序结构与共有骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kleinberg 不可能性定理表明，任何平坦聚类方法都无法同时满足尺度不变性、丰富性和一致性三条自然公理，说明"什么是簇"缺乏统一定义。作者追问：当输出从单一划分变为层次结构时，这种不可能性是否依然存在。

Method: 将三条公理推广到层次聚类情形，证明其可联合满足，并显式构造多种可容许（admissible）方法，例如基于良分离簇的方法以及非二元的单连接（single linkage）变体；进一步在可容许方法类上定义由"细化关系"诱导的偏序。

Result: 可容许方法不可数多；对于某些方法对，一个产生的层次总是细化另一个，由此定义偏序。该偏序集无最大元，且含有不可数多两两不可比较的极大元，显示可容许方法具有显著多样性；但每种可容许方法都包含一个由充分良分离簇构成的层次，任意有限个可容许方法共享一个非平凡的共同骨干。

Conclusion: 层次聚类框架下，Kleinberg 式的三公理不可能性不再成立，可容许方法族丰富但受约束：多样性体现为偏序结构中的极大元，而约束体现为共有骨干的存在。

Abstract: Despite its ubiquity, clustering lacks a universally accepted definition of what is a cluster. Kleinberg's Impossibility Theorem formalizes this difficulty by showing that no flat clustering method can simultaneously satisfy three natural axioms: scale invariance, richness, and consistency. In this paper, we ask whether this impossibility persists when the output is a hierarchy rather than a single partition. We show that, in contrast to the flat clustering setting, the hierarchical analog of these axioms are jointly satisfiable. In fact, there exist uncountably many hierarchical clustering methods satisfying these axioms, which we call admissible. We explicitly construct several admissible methods, including methods based on well-separated clusters and a non-binary version of single linkage. For certain pairs of admissible methods, the hierarchy produced by one always refines that produced by the other. This refinement relation defines a partial order on the class of admissible methods. This partially ordered set has no greatest element and contains uncountably many pairwise incompatible maximal elements, revealing substantial diversity among admissible methods. Nevertheless, this diversity is constrained: every admissible method contains a hierarchy of sufficiently well-separated clusters, and every finite collection of admissible methods shares such a nontrivial common backbone.

</details>


### [234] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现 DR-LabStack——一个 React-Flask Web 系统，将四个异构的糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预训练模型（RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost、Two-level Ensemble）集成为统一临床交互界面，并通过功能测试验证其集成与服务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR 预训练模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理需求和输出语义上存在差异，难以通过统一临床界面访问。因此需要显式协调前端界面与推理服务，构建可复用的异构模型交互与服务流程。

Method: 采用 React 前端与 Flask 后端架构。设计共享表单动态获取有序模型特征，渲染数值与分类控件并构建位置化输入向量；后端适配器加载异构模型工件并应用集成模型附带的 scaler；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类结果展示及方法与来源信息。

Result: 在 2026 年 9 月 8 日的隔离环境中使用复制文件和真实模型工件进行功能评估：四个模型均成功加载并暴露 14、6、8、25 字段契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求表征了服务行为；12 次有限向量检查确认了调用路径与阈值一致性；24 个浏览器组件场景（mock 传输）验证了输入排序、结果渲染及输入校验行为。

Conclusion: DR-LabStack 展示了针对异构 DR 模型的可复用交互与服务流程，其贡献在于 Web 系统设计、集成与软件功能实现；临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需另行评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [235] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiVaT-Fuse：将图像-元数据融合重新定义为样本级隐状态估计的可靠性校准变分张量融合框架，用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度，并在基准上取得最优综合预测排名与更强的扰动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-元数据预测需要融合异质证据，而证据可靠性会随样本与潜在因子变化。现有表示级融合方法通常只选择拼接、门控、条件调制或注意力等聚合结构，却未明确界定在模态不确定性下"融合表示"应当意味着什么，缺乏对融合语义和可靠性建模的显式定义。

Method: RiVaT-Fuse将融合定义为样本级隐状态估计：不通过直接聚合产生融合向量，而是以变分目标估计共识隐状态，平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性四项。用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度，将交互分解为加性、乘性和关系性三部分，并将隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测耦合。论文给出隐状态求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用高效的低秩加对角信任算子实例化该框架。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在直接表示级基线中取得最强综合预测排名，同时在扰动条件下提升了概率稳定性与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 融合应被视为在模态不确定性下的隐状态估计问题而非固定聚合架构的选择；以矩阵值信任几何、结构化交互分解和低秩加对角算子实例化的变分张量融合，能够兼顾预测性能与扰动下的稳定性，为可靠性校准的多模态融合提供了具有理论解释的建模范式。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [236] [Generalized Score Matching for Parameter Estimation on Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11521)
*Nishanth Shetty,Saisuchith Mahajan,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小概率流（MPF）学习出发，构造性地推导了定义在 R^d 凸子集上的广义得分匹配目标，证明其为二阶 proper local scoring rule，并在指数族模型下建立凸性与有限样本估计的相合性，实验验证了在划分函数不可解析求解的约束域上的参数估计与生成建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计需评估并对划分函数求导，对未归一化模型往往不可行；得分匹配虽可绕开归一化常数，但缺乏对一般凸约束域的统一理论框架，且其与归一化常数无关的合理性、正确性保证与统计性质尚待系统阐明。

Method: 以 Minimum Probability Flow（MPF）学习为起点，构造性地推导凸子集上的广义得分匹配目标；说明经典得分匹配与非负数据的域适应变体是该框架的特例；证明该目标为二阶 proper local scoring rule；对指数族模型证明目标凸性及标准正则条件下的有限样本估计相合性；在划分函数解析不可解的约束域上比较基于广义得分匹配的估计量，并进行参数估计与生成建模实验。

Result: 广义得分匹配目标被证明为二阶 proper local scoring rule，最小化可恢复真实密度；指数族模型下目标具凸性且有限样本估计量相合；实验在 R^d 凸子集上的指数族模型密度参数估计及生成建模用例中验证了框架的有效性与更广适用性。

Conclusion: 该推导统一了经典得分匹配与非负数据变体，明确了广义得分匹配的适用范围，为划分函数不可解析求解的约束域学习提供了具理论保证且实用的估计与生成建模框架。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) estimation is a principled and statistically efficient approach for learning probabilistic models. However, for unnormalized models, ML estimation requires evaluating the partition function and differentiating through it, which may not always be tractable. Score matching provides a practically viable alternative that circumvents this obstacle by fitting the score in a way that eliminates dependence on the normalizing constant. We derive the generalized score matching objective on a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ constructively starting from Minimum Probability Flow (MPF) learning, and show how classical score matching as well as domain-adapted variants for non-negative data arise naturally within the proposed framework. We show that the resulting objective is a {\it proper local scoring rule} of second-order, which provides the theoretical guarantee that the true density is recovered when the objective is minimized. Furthermore, for a model belonging to the exponential family, we establish convexity of the objective together with consistency of the finite-sample estimator under standard regularity conditions. Our derivation sheds new light on the scope and applicability of generalized score matching in various problem settings. We compare generalized score matching-based estimators on constrained domains, where the partition function is analytically intractable. We provide experimental results on parameter estimation for model densities belonging to the exponential family defined over convex subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, and a generative modeling use-case to demonstrate broader applicability of the proposed generalized score matching framework.

</details>


### [237] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Epanechnikov核先验贝叶斯小波收缩的去噪框架，用于高噪声自然声景中入侵鸟类的声学分类，SVM在10维MFCC下取得最高0.9398的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声导致鸟类鸣声信号退化，影响入侵物种自动监测与生物入侵管理的可靠性，亟需高效的去噪与分类一体化方案。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法进行信号去噪（具闭式决策规则、计算高效）；对iNaturalist上Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus三种鸟类录音处理；提取MFCC及熵、过零率等频谱特征；比较随机森林、多项逻辑回归与SVM在不同特征维度下的表现。

Result: 小波去噪预处理显著提升分类性能，SVM在10维MFCC配置下取得最高准确率0.9398。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理提供了稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [238] [RDDMPI: Residual Denoising Diffusion Model for Probabilistic Multivariate Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11648)
*Ramiro Valdes Jara,David Chapman,Adam Meyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RDDMPI，一种在残差空间中进行条件扩散的多元时间序列插补框架，通过基线-残差分解简化扩散学习目标，提升插补精度与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数现有扩散式插补方法直接在原始数据空间进行扩散，迫使去噪网络同时捕捉全局结构、时间动态与随机变异，生成任务过于复杂；而现代确定性插补器已能提供较准确的初始重建，因此有理由将扩散过程移至更易建模的残差空间。

Method: RDDMPI将概率插补重构为基线-残差分解：预训练模型捕捉主导信号，扩散过程建模残差不确定性。反向去噪过程以基线补全信号及其隐表示为条件，并引入可靠性感知的条件机制，自适应调节基线信息在残差生成中的影响权重。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，RDDMPI在重建精度和不确定性量化两方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 将扩散过程置于残差空间并利用确定性基线引导，可有效降低扩散学习难度，使模型专注于结构化校正项，为多元时间序列概率插补提供更优方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series imputation (MTSI) aims to recover missing values in temporal data composed of multiple interdependent variables. This problem is central to real-world applications such as healthcare monitoring, traffic networks, and energy systems. Recent diffusion-based approaches have shown strong potential for probabilistic imputation by learning to generate missing values through iterative denoising. However, most existing approaches perform diffusion directly in the original data space, requiring the denoising network to simultaneously capture global structure, temporal dynamics, and stochastic variability. This makes the generative task unnecessarily complex, especially when modern deterministic imputers can already provide accurate initial reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose RDDMPI, a conditional residual diffusion framework that operates directly in residual space. Instead of modeling the full missing signal directly, we reformulate probabilistic imputation as a baseline-residual decomposition, where a pretrained model captures the dominant signal and a diffusion process models the residual uncertainty. To better exploit deterministic guidance, \model{} conditions the reverse denoising process on both the baseline-completed signal and its latent representation, while a reliability-aware conditioning mechanism adaptively controls the influence of baseline information during residual generation. This formulation simplifies the diffusion learning objective, enabling it to focus on structured correction terms rather than reconstructing the full signal. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDDMPI consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [239] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对分布偏移下泛化界理论局限于理想化设定且无法从样本估计的问题，基于熵最优传输提出 $\gamma^{*}$-概念偏移，建立统一协变量偏移与概念偏移的误差界，并给出可估计的估计量与 DataShifts 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习界理论（learning bound theory）适用范围狭窄、过于理想化，且无法从样本中估计，导致理论与实际应用脱节。此外，当源域与目标域支撑集不匹配时，现有概念偏移（concept shift）的定义失效。作者希望弥合理论与实践的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 揭示现有概念偏移定义在源/目标支撑不匹配时失效；2) 借助熵最优传输（entropic optimal transport）提出 $\gamma^{*}$-概念偏移这一关键概念；3) 推导统一协变量偏移与 $\gamma^{*}$-概念偏移的通用误差界，适用于广泛的损失函数、标签空间及随机标注；4) 为该偏移设计具有集中不等式的估计量；5) 提出 DataShifts 算法，用于量化分布偏移并估计误差界。

Result: 提出了统一协变量偏移与 $\gamma^{*}$-概念偏移的通用误差界，覆盖广泛损失函数、标签空间与随机标注；开发了带集中保证（concentration guarantees）的偏移估计量；DataShifts 算法能够在多数应用场景中量化分布偏移并估计误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了分布偏移下学习理论分析与实际应用之间的差距，提供了一个严格且通用的工具，用于分析分布偏移下的学习误差，适用于绝大多数实际应用。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>


### [240] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将认证方法扩展到风险敏感目标，通过在l_p范数有界状态对抗扰动下建立累积奖励指数效用的下界，把风险敏感认证问题转化为凸优化并求其对偶以获得可计算近似，同时提出独立选择训练风险厌恶参数的实证方法。实验表明风险厌恶训练通常获得更高认证下界，且训练风险厌恶程度与认证性能呈非单调关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中部署的强化学习智能体常受状态观测对抗扰动影响，在安全关键应用中带来风险。现有认证方法主要针对风险中性目标，缺乏对风险敏感目标的认证保障，因此需要将认证扩展到风险敏感目标。

Method: 在l_p范数（1≤p<∞）有界状态对抗扰动下，建立累积奖励指数效用的下界。通过引入扰动集的φ-散度松弛，将风险敏感认证问题表述为凸优化，并求其对偶以获得认证下界的可计算近似。此外提出一种实证方法，使训练时的风险厌恶参数β独立于评估时所用的风险水平，以提升认证下界。
